# How to Create AI Love Stories (Full Tutorial)

[@adilinthewild](https://higgsfield.ai/@adilinthewild) Aug 20, 2026

![](https://images.higgs.ai/?default=1&output=webp&url=https%3A%2F%2Fd2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net%2Fuser_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX%2F0da26210-9ddf-4ac1-94f5-611f6b32d483.png&w=1920&q=85)

## Before You Start

Build your assets first — the leads, the giant and the locations repeat in every scene, and locked reference sheets are what keep them consistent from the 1905 harbor to the final frame. Tools used: Seedream 5.0 Pro for character sheets, Soul Cinema for locations, GPT Image 2 for the scale sketch, Seedance 2.5 for every scene. The Claude Skill below writes the video prompts for you: describe the scene like you're telling it to a friend, attach the assets, get back a full shot-by-shot Seedance prompt.

### **DOWNLOAD THE SKILL**

higgsfield-seedance-prompt-builder.mdMD

One trick worth knowing before the prompts: the sheets get you 90% of the way, and the last 10% is a hybrid — keep the generated costume and body, then paste the person's real photo onto the portrait panel. That's what makes the faces read as the actual people instead of lookalikes.

* * *

## Stage 1 — The Assets

### Nate

**What it does:** Generates a three-panel character sheet of the male lead as a 1900s British traveler, face-locked to his real photos.

![v1 (1).png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/d008dabf-14c4-4c3b-93ad-824b44c0c497.png)

Character reference sheet, three views on a seamless neutral gray studio background, evenly lit edge to edge, one uniform tone, no gradient, no vignette: close-up portrait, front full body, back full body. The same young man identical in all panels, face and proportions locked to the attached reference photos.

Identity lock, highest priority: his exact face geometry, bone structure and natural slight asymmetry, dark brown almond-shaped eyes, straight dark brows, his nose shape, lips, defined jawline, ears, light warm skin tone, his hairline. Early-to-mid 20s. Do not reshape, slim, symmetrize or beautify. This lock is bone structure and features only, not clean studio skin. Short dark hair with a natural textured top, softly parted, cropped shorter at the sides, slightly tousled — same hairstyle in all panels.

Skin and stubble, must be visible in the close-up: short dark stubble along the jaw, chin and upper lip, a few days' growth, neat and short. Textured lived-in skin — a light trace of road dust on the cheekbones and jaw, faint weathering, skin that has seen life. Kept light: not dirty, not grimy, no soot, just real. No clean polished studio skin.

Outfit: 1900s Britain, middle-class. Stylish everyday menswear, a textured voluminous layered silhouette — styling entirely at your discretion within the era. Clean and well-fitted, just softly broken-in from travel, a hint of a free-spirited wanderer in how he wears it. Not wealthy, not ragged. Identical outfit in every panel.

Panels: close-up — head and shoulders, full front, both ears visible, nose centered, eyes down the lens; an interesting deep gaze, slightly sly, subtly ironic, a barely-there half-smile — refined, sharp-minded, perceptive, quiet intelligence behind the eyes. Front — full body, relaxed confident standing pose facing camera, both hands visible. Back — full body facing directly away. Full-body panels at identical scale, feet on one ground line.

LIGHT — SOFT AND SHADOWLESS: completely even wrap-around diffused daylight, as under a full overcast sky or inside a softbox dome — light arrives from every direction equally, with no readable light direction at all. No shadows anywhere: none under the brows, nose, chin or collar, none in the clothing folds beyond their own fabric tone, no cast shadows on the background, no drop shadow behind him. No highlights, no shine, no hotspots on the skin — matte, even, natural skin texture, low contrast, true-to-life colors, identical exposure across all three panels.

Shot on 35mm film, Kodak Vision3 500T: fine organic grain, soft cinematic contrast, no digital sharpness.

Same man in all panels, only angle and crop change. Both hands whole. No plastic CGI skin, no identity change, no clothing change between panels, no on-image text or labels. 21:9.

### Aila

**What it does:** Generates her matching three-panel sheet — a late-1800s traveler with hat, cape and suitcase, face-locked to her photos.

![d8027661-44ba-4ee3-9a12-9c57e5509f49.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/5a711d78-625e-4dda-a522-57325b59495c.png)

Character reference sheet, three views on a seamless neutral light-grey studio background: close-up portrait, front full body, back full body. The same young woman identical in all panels, face and proportions locked to the attached reference photos.

Identity lock, highest priority: her exact face geometry, bone structure and natural slight asymmetry, dark brown eyes, strong full dark brows, her nose shape, full lips, soft rounded jawline, warm light-olive skin, her small beauty marks kept in place. Early 20s, bare natural face. Do not reshape, slim, symmetrize or beautify. This lock is bone structure and features only. Long thick dark-brown wavy hair worn loose under the hat, falling well past her shoulders, soft strands framing the face, same in all panels.

Skin, visible in the close-up: natural, lightly textured living skin — a soft healthy freshness, faint sea-air flush on the cheeks, a traveler at the start of a journey; not weathered, not dirty. No clean polished retouch skin.

Outfit, identical in every panel — late-1800s young traveler arriving at a port, modest and neat, the whole palette a soft, warm LIGHT BEIGE gently muted with grey: BEIGE leads, grey only softens it — a cozy soft-beige greige, clearly warm to the eye, the cloak and hat reading as light beige with a grey whisper — never a strong saturated beige, never brown, and never a grey-dominant or cold palette:

\- a wide-brimmed period traveling HAT in pale grey-beige woven fabric, a low soft crown wrapped with a slate-grey ribbon band tied in a loose bow, a few soft muted feathers tucked at the side; worn level, the face fully visible

\- a long soft WOOL traveling CLOAK in light grey-beige (greige) — a full calf-length cape with a layered shoulder capelet, draping in deep soft folds, fastened at the throat with a thin darker ribbon tie; matte, gently worn from travel, not new-looking

\- under it a cream high-collared period BLOUSE with a ruffled lace-trimmed front placket and full sleeves gathered into buttoned cuffs

\- a long charcoal-grey wool SKIRT falling to the ankle, simple and practical, with a small waistband detail

\- warm brown knit STOCKINGS visible above the shoes

\- brown leather lace-up period SHOES with a low heel, lightly scuffed from the road

\- in one hand a small vintage travel SUITCASE: pale canvas sides with brown leather trim, corner caps and small brass studs and latches — compact, well-traveled

Everything modest, practical, softly lived-in; textured wool, cotton and leather; nothing costume-bright, nothing shiny.

Panels:

\- Left, CLOSE-UP with CHARACTER: head and shoulders in a three-quarter view, her face turned slightly away from camera but her eyes back into the lens, hat on, face clearly visible and matching the references; a warm, gentle half-smile, open curious eyes with quiet excitement — a modest young traveler arriving somewhere new: hopeful, soft, self-possessed. The cloak's collar and ribbon tie visible at her shoulders.

\- Center, FULL-BODY FRONT: head to shoes, facing camera, weight balanced, the cloak falling in full folds around her, the suitcase held in one hand at her side, the other hand relaxed and visible.

\- Right, FULL-BODY BACK: head to shoes, facing directly away, the cloak's back drape and her long wavy hair down over it, the hat's back, the suitcase in the same hand at her side.

Full-body panels at identical scale, feet on one ground line.

Soft diffused natural daylight, no artificial lights, no highlights or shine on the face. Matte, even, natural skin texture, low contrast, soft muted shadows, true-to-life colors — the palette reading soft warm light-beige, gently greyed — beige clearly leading, softly warm, never a strong beige, never brown, never grey-dominant or cold. Shot on 35mm film, Kodak Vision3 500T: fine organic grain, gentle halation, soft cinematic contrast, no digital sharpness.

Same woman in all panels, only angle and crop change. Both hands whole. No plastic CGI skin, no identity change, no clothing change between panels, the suitcase in every full-body panel, no on-image text or labels. 21:9.

### The Harbor

**What it does:** Generates the empty 1905 pier master plate with the sun pinned in place, so the light never jumps between generations.

![hf_20260801_183505_3702285e-21df-46e0-944e-0e084726ee9f.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/24c8cb1b-d6ea-405b-a981-7fd077f0d95f.png)

Master location plate, completely empty of people, 2.39:1.

A busy early-1900s working harbor in soft hazy afternoon light, minutes before a great liner departs.

GEOMETRY:

1\. PIER — a long wide timber pier runs from the bottom foreground straight into the depth, dead ahead to the ship at its far end. Iron bollards and coiled ropes line its edges; small waves slap the pilings below.

2\. LINER — at the pier's end, in three-quarter bow view slightly right of the pier's axis: black hull low on the water, white superstructure crowded with deck rails, THREE tall black-and-white banded funnels pouring dark coal smoke that drifts across the pale sky. A heavy anchor chain of thick oval links hangs from the bow hawse toward the water — wet rust over black paint, clearly readable. A wooden boarding gangway with rope-and-iron railings rises from the pier end to an open doorway in the hull side, warm light inside it.

3\. FLANKS — left: dense stacks of wooden crates, barrels, sacks and cargo nets. Right: tall timber A-frame loading cranes with ropes, hooks and hanging chains, more bollards. Beyond both edges of the pier, tall-masted sailing ships lie moored on either side — a forest of masts, spars and rigging dissolving into the sea haze. Strung telegraph lines cross the sky overhead.

ATMOSPHERE: light sea haze softening every depth plane; the coal smoke shears slowly with the breeze; a few gulls wheel between the masts.

LIGHT: soft hazy afternoon sun from high side-back, warm-neutral; long soft shadows from crates and cranes across the worn planks; the sky pale and milky with haze; the open doorway and portholes hold faint warm glow.

PHYSICS: the liner sits deep and motionless, whitecaps breaking against its bow and the pilings; rigging lines sway faintly; rope fiber, wood grain, flaked paint and wet rust readable on every surface.

OPTICS: Arri Alexa Mini LF with anamorphic primes, 2.39:1; camera slightly elevated, about 4 m, at the near end of the pier, looking straight down its axis to the liner; edges gently soft, center sharp; fine organic 35mm grain.

STYLE: photorealistic early-1900s period-drama cinematography, grounded and unpolished, no text or signage anywhere.

### The Scale Sketch

**What it does:** Generates a pencil scale sketch of the giant and the boy; fed back as a reference, it locks the height difference in every batch.

![hf_20260805_220717_8784cc00-d63a-4cd5-8f40-6e6ce64a235f.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/d3b7ff59-4bea-475e-9aea-f96b79c0c60c.png)

Using @8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6 and @c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0 as character references, create a hand-drawn concept sketch: a height comparison chart of the two characters standing side by side, both in full body FRONT view, on a plain white background.

Style: rough hand-drawn line art — confident sketchy contour lines in dark pencil or fine ink, loose and energetic like a film production concept sketch; line work only, no color, no painted shading, at most a few sparse hatching strokes for volume; visible construction lines and slightly imperfect strokes, authentic sketchbook feel. No text, no labels, no measurement lines, no letters or numbers anywhere.

Composition and scale — the core of the image, the single most important instruction: both characters stand on the same ground line, facing the viewer, a small gap between them. The GIANT from @8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6 is a colossus THREE times the height of the young warrior — the young warrior's entire body stacks three times into the giant's height. The hard checkpoint: the top of the young warrior's head reaches only the giant's MID-THIGH, just below the hem of the giant's ragged wrap — NOT his waist, NOT his chest. The young warrior from @c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0 stands at normal human height, tiny beside the colossus, occupying only the lower third of the frame while the giant fills it top to bottom. This extreme difference is intentional and must be kept exactly: do NOT balance the figures, do NOT enlarge the warrior, do NOT shrink the difference toward realistic proportions — if the warrior's head reaches the giant's waist or higher, the scale is WRONG.

The giant executioner, drawn from @8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6: the enormous soft-heavy silhouette — a huge round belly, broad soft chest with dense body hair suggested by loose scribbled strokes, thick heavy arms and column legs, no muscle definition, the mass of a colossal fat giant; his head fully covered by the peaked leather executioner's hood with its round stitched eye holes and flaring skirt over the shoulders — a cold heavy stare through the holes, no visible face; TWO massive chains of fist-sized links crossing his torso in a big X — over both shoulders, crossing on the chest-belly, no loose hanging ends; a ragged jagged-hemmed hide wrap around the hips; in his right hand a plain single-bladed executioner's axe held low, the broad crescent blade beside his shin; cloth-wrapped sandals. The hood, the X of chains and the axe simplified into bold confident strokes.

The young warrior, drawn from @c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0: the lean athletic silhouette in the muscled bronze cuirass over the short pleated skirt with a frayed hem, a baldric strap across the chest, bronze bracers on the forearms, the xiphos sword held low in one hand with the point angled down, strapped sandals laced high up the shins, short dark hair — his calm face with a light closed-lip half-smile captured in minimal clean lines.

Both figures in relaxed standing poses, clearly readable silhouettes. Clean white paper background, the sketch centered with generous margin above the giant's head so nothing is cropped. 16:9.

### The Carnival Square

**What it does:** Generates the night carnival piazza with the costumed crowd baked into the location itself.

![hf_20260801_173044_184dcbc8-5b46-4e78-8a76-cc2967b15b94.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/2eab095f-11c8-41c1-8b9b-330bf6f6172b.png)

LOCATION REFERENCE — OLD ITALIAN CARNIVAL SQUARE, ALIVE WITH PEOPLE. No lead characters — background crowd only.

Scene:

A grand night carnival in the heart of old Italy — a wide open central piazza paved with worn smooth stone slabs, holding a full festival in progress. Along one side of the square rises a magnificent old Italian PALAZZO — a renaissance-baroque palace facade of aged warm stone with tall arched windows glowing from within, ornate balconies draped with heraldic banners, a grand arched entrance portal with wide stone steps descending to the square. The other sides of the piazza are framed by old Italian architecture: arcaded galleries with columns, weathered ochre and terracotta facades with green shutters, a stone fountain, a slender campanile tower against the night sky. Strings of festival lanterns and garlands criss-cross high above the square between the buildings. FIREWORKS burst over the palazzo roofline — big gold and warm-white blooms against the deep blue-black night sky — and golden ground spark fountains hiss at the square's corners.

The crowd:

The piazza is PACKED with a festive costumed throng — a mass of people in classic Venetian carnival attire: bauta and colombina masks, gilded and painted full-face masks, tricorn hats, hooded capes and cloaks, jesters with bells, plague-doctor masks, powdered wigs, feathered headdresses, embroidered doublets and opulent gowns. People dance in circles on the open center of the square, stroll in masked pairs under the lantern strings, cluster in laughing groups by the arcades and the fountain, raise glasses on the palazzo steps, applaud the fireworks. Torch-bearers and lantern-carriers thread through the crowd. Everyone is in motion — no frozen figures. Costumes in rich saturated jewel and warm festival tones: emerald, sapphire, deep violet, teal, gold, silver, ochre, cream — ABSOLUTELY no red, no burgundy, no wine tones on any costume, mask or banner anywhere in the frame. Every person has a unique individual face or mask, unique build and costume — no duplicated figures.

Atmosphere / Lighting:

NIGHT, the square ablaze with celebration. All illumination from motivated practicals: the criss-crossing lantern strings overhead, torches and iron wall-lanterns on the facades, warm light pouring from the palazzo's windows and portal, candle-lit arcades, the firework blooms washing the square in golden pulses, spark fountains glittering. Warm festive haze drifting over the crowd, gentle night breeze in the garlands and banners. Every face and costume readable, no crushed shadows. Color: dominant warm festival gold on stone and crowd, secondary deep blue-black night sky, accents of jewel-tone costumes and banner heraldry.

Camera:

Wide establishing general plan from a slightly elevated 3/4 angle — camera raised a few meters above the square, as if from a balcony at one corner, rotated roughly 45° to the palazzo: the palace facade recedes diagonally on one side of frame, the arcaded square and campanile on the other, the packed celebrating crowd filling the open piazza between them, fireworks blooming in the sky above the palazzo roofline. Foreground and mid-field sharp; depth falloff only on the far rooftops and sky.

Style / Quality:

35mm anamorphic film still: horizontal squeeze and compression, oval / elliptical bokeh with stretched highlights on the lanterns and torch flames, curved barrel edge distortion, chromatic aberration toward the edges — all subtle-to-moderate, gentle. NO lens flares, NO streaks, NO floating bokeh circles. Photorealistic, 8K detail. Era-authentic old-Italy materials: aged stone, patinated bronze, weathered stucco, worn paving polished by centuries. Real crowd physics: capes and gowns moving, torch flames alive, correct contact shadows on the stone.

## Stage 2 — The Prompting Framework

Here's the workflow that turns a scene idea into a ready-to-run Seedance prompt. Download the skill, then in Claude go to **Customize → Skills** and upload it. Open a fresh chat, attach your script and every asset you built in Stage 1, and give Claude the element list — the names Claude uses in the prompt must match the names of your assets. Then in Higgsfield add each asset under **Elements** with exactly the same names, and drop the finished prompt into Cinema Studio.

higgsfield-seedance-prompt-builder.mdMD

`@Nate_cruise / @Aila_cruise — the harbor-era character sheets
@cruise — the harbor location plate
@giant — the executioner sheet
@arena, @throne — the arena location assets
@sketch — the height comparison sketch
@Nate_train / @Aila_train, @train — the western-era sheets and railway
@storyboard1, @storyboard2, @storyboard3 — the hand-drawn shot sketches
@Nate_ship / @Aila_ship, @duelist_ship, @ship, @deck, @spyglass, @door, @crew — the pirate assets
@Nate_carnival / @Aila_carnival, @carnival — the carnival assets
@Nate_elevator / @Aila_elavator, @elavator, @headphones, @Audio 1 — the modern-day assets`

* * *

## Stage 3 — The Scenes

### Scene 1 — The Harbor

**What it does:** Animates the full 11-shot harbor sequence, from the seagull fly-in to the leap after the departing liner.

**Assets:** @Nate\_cruise, @Aila\_cruise, @cruise

**Style header:** Reuse the technical block at the top of this prompt in every scene — same optics, same grade — and six different eras cut together as one film.

![d8027661-44ba-4ee3-9a12-9c57e5509f49.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/28903441-5150-492b-9930-2b3c97f5c9b1.png)

![v1 (1).png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/5e0a2da4-2bfc-42de-b44f-ce7ad30efe88.png)

![hf_20260801_183505_3702285e-21df-46e0-944e-0e084726ee9f.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/985e119c-473f-4959-a43e-981f2c050b1d.png)

11 shots, 22 seconds total, 2.39:1, anamorphic. Hard cuts — fast-cut rhythm. Slow motion ONLY inside SHOT 4 and inside SHOT 11 from the takeoff. One continuous story: a chance collision on a departure pier — a man falls in love in one look, lets her go, understands, and leaps.

SCENE ENGINE (production spine — the whole piece plays this chain)

GOAL (fixed, never changes): reach HER before the ship takes her — fix the mistake made one minute ago, when he let the encounter end.

OBSTACLE (global): the liner is leaving NOW — every second of the piece the goal is dying; (local): the packed pier, the crossing cart, the widening water gap.

TACTIC (search under incomplete knowledge): first he files the encounter away and walks off — a reasonable move for a man who doesn't yet know it was love; the failure of that tactic RETURNS INFORMATION — the sum won't balance, and the horn closes the truth gap. The threat then forces the only tactics left: the sprint, the vault, the leap.

REVERSAL (his own action flips on him): the walk he took to keep his dignity becomes the distance he must now sprint back; the man who let her go becomes the man leaping off a pier.

VALUE SHIFT (the audience's verdict moves): passer-by → polite romantic → sensible man who walks away → no — he's ALIVE, he's in love → madman → that's what a man is. Her verdict: stranger → cool beauty → no, she's interested.

REFERENCE DEFINITIONS

@3399ef3b-e4f4-41c5-a288-d354a85168c0: working harbor pier, early 1900s — a long wide plank pier running to a great ocean liner at its end, dark hull, funnels pouring smoke into the hazy pale sky; bollards, nets, coiled ropes, crates and hand-carts along both edges; dock cranes and a forest of moored sailing-ship masts across the water, far hills; gulls in the haze. Geometry, materials and atmosphere only. Reference.

@109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad: young man — face, hair and wardrobe match the reference exactly in every frame: knee-length brown wool coat with notch lapels worn OPEN, dark-brown wool waistcoat, off-white band-collar shirt, brown wool trousers, worn brown leather lace-up boots, short dark side-parted hair, hands EMPTY, no hat. Reference.

@8fc5d081-17fc-4ac7-9d97-9599bb6300f2: young woman — face, hair and wardrobe match the reference exactly in every frame: pale fabric hat with a dark ribbon that STAYS ON her head, long dark wavy hair, sage-grey long cape tied at the throat, cream high-collar lace blouse, grey ankle-length skirt, brown heeled ankle boots, a small pale canvas suitcase with leather corners in her LEFT hand — the LEFT hand in every frame she walks. Reference.

TECHNICAL BLOCK

Cinematic photoreal, ultra filmic early-1900s harbor pier. 21:9 — 35mm anamorphic film character: horizontal squeeze and compression, oval elliptical bokeh with stretched highlights, curved barrel edge distortion, subtle chromatic aberration toward the edges — all subtle and moderate; NO lens flares, NO streaks, NO floating bokeh circles. SOFT COLOR GRADE: gentle muted warm pastels, cream highlights, lifted soft blacks, low contrast — soft and faded. Shot on 35mm, fine period grain, hazy warm backlit daylight, sun soft behind the drifting funnel smoke — light identical first frame to last. Natural 180-degree shutter cadence — directional motion blur on fast moves, readable poses at holds; no ghosting, no duplicated limbs, no strobing. ALL HANDHELD, organic, never chaotic; SHOT 1 rides a smooth airborne glide, SHOT 11 a static level profile stage. SFX only.

LAWS:

— LOCATION LOCK: every pier shot — SHOTS 1–9 and SHOT 11 — is the SAME @3399ef3b-e4f4-41c5-a288-d354a85168c0 pier: plank geometry, bollards, crates, dock cranes, moored masts, far hills, funnel smoke and haze identical throughout; SHOT 10 is the deck of that same liner.

— FACE RULE: a lead's visible face fills at least a THIRD of frame height, chest-up or closer, fully inside the frame; at distance a lead reads from behind or in profile; eyes never look into the lens.

— LIVING FACE: the eyes always WORK at a task, never pose; a blink lands ONLY on a change of thought, slow and soft, a few per shot; never a frozen glassy stare. His eyes stay DRY throughout.

— FACE STABILITY: both faces photoreal, sharp, identity-locked every frame — no warping, in motion and slow motion alike.

— PROPS: HE carries nothing — both hands empty every frame; SHE carries exactly ONE small suitcase, only in her LEFT hand, low at her side.

— NO GLOW: no halo — sun draws only a thin realistic edge; shadow sides honest, never crushed.

— PORTRAIT DISTANCE: close framings come from a longer lens at distance — natural proportions, no wide-angle facial distortion.

— LEVEL CAMERA: horizon level, zero roll, verticals upright.

— LIVING CROWD (every frame on the pier): the pier is PACKED, each figure MID-ACTION every second — embraces, trunks, children darting, hand-carts, dockworkers, a fish crate trailed by gulls; density never drops, every body solid, nobody looks at the leads; passers-by wipe close past the lens every few seconds. In SHOTS 6–9 @8fc5d081-17fc-4ac7-9d97-9599bb6300f2 is never visible anywhere.

— SHIP & CHAIN (SHOT 11): the liner is UNDER WAY, sliding away past the pier's end, nothing connecting it to shore; the anchor chain hangs as ONE FREE VERTICAL LINE from the bow hawse straight down to the water, plumb, winched slowly up, links rising wet — oval links about thirty centimeters, forearm-thick, the chain narrower than his shoulders; no hooks, never diagonal, never touching the pier. The sea is fine irregular chop — flickering sparkle, wobbling reflections, no stripes, no patterns, never glassy.

SHOT 1 — 0.0–4.5s — ARRIVING WITH THE GULL · geography

84–100° aerial glide. First frame: open sky and haze — a single white GULL sweeps in beside the camera and the camera FLIES WITH IT, descending at 45 degrees over the packed artery of the @3399ef3b-e4f4-41c5-a288-d354a85168c0 pier: the passenger current flowing toward the glowing liner, crates heaved along the edges, a hand-cart cutting a seam, children darting, long soft shadows. The gull banks away over the crowd as the shot ends. No lead identifiable.

SHOT 2 — 4.5–5.5s — HIS BOOTS · HER BOOTS

Hard cut. Ankle height, back-tracking: @109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad's boots stride FAST at the camera, firm heel-to-toe, dust puffing — the only boots against the current. Mid-shot hard cut: @8fc5d081-17fc-4ac7-9d97-9599bb6300f2's boots hurry at the camera WITH the flow — quick light steps, hem swaying, the pale suitcase corner swinging at her LEFT side.

SHOT 3 — 5.5–6.5s — THE HIT

Hard cut, shoulder height, close. STAGING: @109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad enters frame-RIGHT (x≈70%) moving screen-left, chest and face toward HER; @8fc5d081-17fc-4ac7-9d97-9599bb6300f2 enters frame-LEFT (x≈30%) moving screen-right, chest and face toward HIM, suitcase in her LEFT hand — both within one meter of the lens, chests facing each other, never backs. Within half a second their right shoulders CLIP beside the camera: the suitcase swings wide once, her cape flares, his torso twists a quarter-turn, one faltered half-step each, a sharp intake of breath. PHYSICS

Both leads hurry with real weight; the collision has true mass — torsos rotate, one faltered half-step each, never backward; the full turns pivot on planted feet. His heavy walk carries honest weight; the dead stop lands on a planted heel; the whip pivot turns head-first with motion blur; the sprint explodes through real acceleration and holds full tilt. The vault is true parkour physics — the palm takes real weight, hips swing clean, the landing leg rolls back into stride, the cart rocks, nothing tips. The leap is true ballistics — one driving-leg takeoff, a shallow rising arc, no floating, no mid-air correction; slow motion stretches time, never the trajectory. The chain hangs plumb under its own weight, rising at a steady winch pace, shedding scattered heavy drops. Cloth, hair and the suitcase carry natural delay, exaggerated only in slow motion; every figure solid; the gull flies with real wing mechanics.

SFX only: from the air — wind, the gull's wingbeats and one cry, the dock's wash rising with the descent — dropping INTO the crowd: layered boots, urgent murmur, crate thuds, cart wheels, gulls; his firm strikes and her quick light steps; the soft cloth THUD of the hit, a sharp intake of breath — the world dipping muffled through the slow-motion turn, one slowed gull cry, a single close breath of each — crashing back as she leaves; farewells washing under his held breath, his slowing steps — the LONG DEEP HORN over everything, one caught breath in the near-silence, the whip of cloth on the pivot — the rapid hammer of the sprint, grinding cart wheels, the slap of his palm, the double thud of the vault — one beat of high sea wind and her amused exhale at the rail — boots on the last planks, the deep wash of the moving hull, the rhythmic clank of the windlass — the plank's creak under the plant — and the world falls away with the leap into a soft airy hush: wind, heartbeat, one slowed gull cry, water flicking off the rising links — the piece ends inside the held breath of the reach. No dialogue, no music. 35mm grain, soft faded period-drama realism.

ERE+SHE+WAS+GOING%0AHard+cut%2C+real+speed+crashes+back.+Camera+just+behind+%3C%3C%3C109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad%3E%3E%3E%27s+right+shoulder%2C+his+jaw+soft+in+the+near-right+foreground%3A+%3C%3C%3C8fc5d081-17fc-4ac7-9d97-9599bb6300f2%3E%3E%3E+breaks+the+look+FIRST+%E2%80%94+turns+and+hurries+on+the+way+she+was+always+going%2C+toward+the+ship%2C+back+to+camera%2C+suitcase+in+her+LEFT+hand%2C+weaving+through+gaps%3B+passers-by+wipe+the+near+foreground+and+after+each+wipe+she+is+farther%2C+her+hat+sinking+between+shoulders.+She+does+not+look+back.+He+stays+planted%2C+turned+after+her.%0ASHOT+6+%E2%80%94+10.5%E2%80%9313.5s+%E2%80%94+HE+WATCHES+HER+GO+%C2%B7+the+turn+%C2%B7+his+back+to+the+ship+%C2%B7+the+heavy+walk%0AHard+cut%3A+close+slight+three-quarter+on+%3C%3C%3C109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad%3E%3E%3E+from+ahead-right%2C+47%E2%80%9363%C2%B0%2C+the+living+crowd+soft+at+every+depth.%0AACTING+TASK+%E2%80%94+HIM%3A+scene+direction+%28the+whole+pier+lives+it%29%3A+partings+are+the+way+of+things+%E2%80%94+you+let+people+go+and+get+on+with+your+day.+His+motive%3A+a+grown+man+doesn%27t+chase+a+stranger%27s+ship.+His+goal%3A+file+the+encounter+away+and+walk+off+clean.+His+tactic+%E2%80%94+ESCORT+her+with+his+eyes+as+long+as+the+crowd+allows+%28smooth+pursuit%2C+a+fraction+of+head+drift%29%2C+hold+the+empty+spot+half+a+breath+when+the+crowd+takes+her%2C+one+slow+blink+exactly+on+the+loss+%E2%80%94+then+the+head+DIPS%2C+he+TURNS+and+walks+with+his+BACK+to+the+liner%2C+slowly+toward+the+camera%2C+eyes+down%2C+working+the+planks+like+a+man+re-checking+an+arithmetic+that+won%27t+come+out+even%3B+each+step+re-closes+the+drawer+and+each+step+it+swings+back+open%3B+his+pace+loses+a+fraction+per+step.+The+camera+back-tracks+glued+to+the+framing.%0ASHOT+7+%E2%80%94+13.5%E2%80%9315.5s+%E2%80%94+THE+HORN+%C2%B7+HE+REALIZES+%C2%B7+THE+PIVOT%0ASame+setup%2C+tighter+%E2%80%94+%3C%3C%3C109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad%3E%3E%3E%27s+face+large+in+three-quarter%2C+the+liner+far+behind+his+back.+A+LONG+DEEP+HORN+rolls+over+the+pier+%E2%80%94+and+the+truth+gap+CLOSES%3A+he+has+fallen+in+love%2C+and+he+is+LOSING+her%2C+right+now%2C+to+that+ship.+His+step+DIES+mid-stride%3B+the+eyes+SNAP+up+off+the+planks+and+go+still+%E2%80%94+breath+catches+and+shortens%2C+jaw+sets%2C+one+knocked-out+blink%3B+a+half-turn+of+the+head+back+toward+the+ship+%E2%80%94+the+eyes+sharpen%2C+decided+%E2%80%94+he+WHIPS+around+over+his+left+shoulder%2C+head+snapping+first+with+real+motion+blur%2C+body+following+on+a+planted+boot%2C+coat+flaring+%E2%80%94+and+explodes+into+the+sprint+after+her.+The+face+never+performs+%E2%80%94+the+task+does+everything.%0ASHOT+8+%E2%80%94+15.5%E2%80%9316.5s+%E2%80%94+THE+CART+VAULT+%C2%B7+head-on%0AHard+cut.+LOW+back-tracking+in+front+of+%3C%3C%3C109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad%3E%3E%3E%2C+knee+height%2C+real+speed+%E2%80%94+a+loaded+HAND-CART+cuts+across+from+frame-left%2C+its+puller+leaning+into+the+shafts+%E2%80%94+he+does+NOT+slow%3A+two+loading+strides%2C+he+LEAPS%2C+one+palm+tapping+the+top+crate%2C+boots+swinging+clean+OVER+the+load+toward+the+camera%2C+coat+flying%2C+dust+bursting+as+he+LANDS+without+breaking+stride+%E2%80%94+the+cart-man%27s+head+snaps+after+him.%0ASHOT+9+%E2%80%94+16.5%E2%80%9317.5s+%E2%80%94+THE+RUN+%C2%B7+close+three-quarter%0AHard+cut.+Camera+back-tracks+fast+ahead-right+of+him%2C+47%E2%80%9363%C2%B0%3A+%3C%3C%3C109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad%3E%3E%3E%27s+face+LARGE+in+three-quarter%2C+breath+tearing%2C+arms+driving%2C+jaw+carved+by+the+backlight%3B+the+crowd+blurs+behind+his+far+shoulder%2C+heads+turning+after+him.%0AACTING+TASK%3A+his+eyes+CATCH+THE+SHIP+beyond+the+camera+and+hold+it+like+a+rope+%E2%80%94+measuring+the+shrinking+distance+stride+by+stride%2C+recalculating+the+path+through+the+crowd+without+letting+go.%0ASHOT+10+%E2%80%94+17.5%E2%80%9318.5s+%E2%80%94+HER+PROFILE+AT+THE+RAIL+%C2%B7+lightly+nonchalant%0AHard+cut+%E2%80%94+high+on+the+ship%3A+%3C%3C%3C8fc5d081-17fc-4ac7-9d97-9599bb6300f2%3E%3E%3E%27s+clean+PROFILE+close-up+at+the+deck+rail+against+the+open+hazy+golden+sky%2C+hat+line+sharp+against+the+light%2C+sea+wind+lifting+her+hair+back%2C+one+hand+resting+easy+on+the+worn+rail.%0AACTING+TASK+%E2%80%94+HER%3A+task+%E2%80%94+appraise+the+running+stranger+below+without+giving+anything+away%3B+her+eyes+angle+DOWN+to+the+pier+and+snag+on+the+running+figure%3A+a+soft+%C2%ABhuh%C2%BB+of+an+exhale+through+the+nose%2C+the+visible+corner+of+her+mouth+curling+into+a+faint+warm+half-smile%2C+one+slow+amused+narrowing+of+the+eye+%E2%80%94+cool+on+the+surface%2C+a+spark+of+real+interest+underneath+%E2%80%94+and+she+keeps+watching%2C+the+little+smile+holding+in+profile.+One+soft+blink+on+the+moment+she+notices.+No+big+emotion.%0ASHOT+11+%E2%80%94+18.5%E2%80%9322.0s+%E2%80%94+THE+FINAL+SPRINT+AND+THE+LEAP+%C2%B7+static+profile%0AHard+cut.+One+breath+of+the+sprint+from+behind-side%3A+the+last+bare+planks+of+the+%3C%3C%3C3399ef3b-e4f4-41c5-a288-d354a85168c0%3E%3E%3E+pier+rushing+under+%3C%3C%3C109ff858-6a87-47ab-8bea-5e5088c076ad%3E%3E%3E%27s+boots%2C+the+towering+dark+bow+sliding+AWAY+past+the+pier%27s+end%2C+the+wet+anchor+chain+climbing+link+by+link+out+of+the+choppy+water+%E2%80%94+he+BURSTS%2C+head+dropping+into+full+commitment+%E2%80%94+then+the+STATIC+profile+stage%2C+level%2C+perpendicular%2C+1.5+meters+above+the+water.+STAGING%3A+the+pier+edge+lower-LEFT+with+its+empty+planks%3B+the+vertical+chain+at+x%E2%89%8865%25+falling+from+the+top+edge+to+the+wet+anchor+at+the+water+%28y%E2%89%8875%25%29%3B+the+dark+hull+edge+slicing+the+extreme+frame-RIGHT%2C+gliding+away%3B+cranes+and+moored+ships+far+in+the+haze%3B+his+flight+crosses+the+CENTER+at+the+upper+third%2C+left+to+right.+His+last+stride+PLANTS+on+the+edge+plank+%E2%80%94+it+flexes%2C+dust+kicks+%E2%80%94+and+EXACTLY+on+the+takeoff+the+shot+ramps+into+smooth+slow+motion%3A+one+short+committed+spring+over+the+choppy+gap%2C+body+stretching+fully+HORIZONTAL+like+a+swimmer%2C+coat+flaring+behind%2C+one+white+gull+gliding+above+the+arc+%E2%80%94+both+arms+driving+forward%2C+hands+OPEN%2C+fingers+spreading+toward+the+wet+links+just+above+the+anchor+%E2%80%94+closer+%E2%80%94+centimeters.+Final+frame+HELD%3A+full+wide+profile%2C+mid-air+at+the+top+of+the+reach%2C+hands+a+palm%27s+width+from+the+rising+iron%2C+spray+suspended%2C+the+gull+mid-wingbeat.+This+exact+frame+is+the+last+frame.%0APHYSICS%0ABoth+leads+hurry+with+real+weight%3B+the+collision+has+true+mass+%E2%80%94+torsos+rotate%2C+one+faltered+half-step+each%2C+never+backward%3B+the+full+turns+pivot+on+planted+feet.+His+heavy+walk+carries+honest+weight%3B+the+dead+stop+lands+on+a+planted+heel%3B+the+whip+pivot+turns+head-first+with+motion+blur%3B+the+sprint+explodes+through+real+acceleration+and+holds+full+tilt.+The+vault+is+true+parkour+physics+%E2%80%94+the+palm+takes+real+weight%2C+hips+swing+clean%2C+the+landing+leg+rolls+back+into+stride%2C+the+cart+rocks%2C+nothing+tips.+The+leap+is+true+ballistics+%E2%80%94+one+driving-leg+takeoff%2C+a+shallow+rising+arc%2C+no+floating%2C+no+mid-air+correction%3B+slow+motion+stretches+time%2C+never+the+trajectory.+The+chain+hangs+plumb+under+its+own+weight%2C+rising+at+a+steady+winch+pace%2C+shedding+scattered+heavy+drops.+Cloth%2C+hair+and+the+suitcase+carry+natural+delay%2C+exaggerated+only+in+slow+motion%3B+every+figure+solid%3B+the+gull+flies+with+real+wing+mechanics.%0ASFX+only%3A+from+the+air+%E2%80%94+wind%2C+the+gull%27s+wingbeats+and+one+cry%2C+the+dock%27s+wash+rising+with+the+descent+%E2%80%94+dropping+INTO+the+crowd%3A+layered+boots%2C+urgent+murmur%2C+crate+thuds%2C+cart+wheels%2C+gulls%3B+his+firm+strikes+and+her+quick+light+steps%3B+the+soft+cloth+THUD+of+the+hit%2C+a+sharp+intake+of+breath+%E2%80%94+the+world+dipping+muffled+through+the+slow-motion+turn%2C+one+slowed+gull+cry%2C+a+single+close+breath+of+each+%E2%80%94+crashing+back+as+she+leaves%3B+farewells+washing+under+his+held+breath%2C+his+slowing+steps+%E2%80%94+the+LONG+DEEP+HORN+over+everything%2C+one+caught+breath+in+the+near-silence%2C+the+whip+of+cloth+on+the+pivot+%E2%80%94+the+rapid+hammer+of+the+sprint%2C+grinding+cart+wheels%2C+the+slap+of+his+palm%2C+the+double+thud+of+the+vault+%E2%80%94+one+beat+of+high+sea+wind+and+her+amused+exhale+at+the+rail+%E2%80%94+boots+on+the+last+planks%2C+the+deep+wash+of+the+moving+hull%2C+the+rhythmic+clank+of+the+windlass+%E2%80%94+the+plank%27s+creak+under+the+plant+%E2%80%94+and+the+world+falls+away+with+the+leap+into+a+soft+airy+hush%3A+wind%2C+heartbeat%2C+one+slowed+gull+cry%2C+water+flicking+off+the+rising+links+%E2%80%94+the+piece+ends+inside+the+held+breath+of+the+reach.+No+dialogue%2C+no+music.+35mm+grain%2C+soft+faded+period-drama+realism.)

* * *

### Scene 2 — The Fight

**What it does:** Animates the giant fight — the throw, the sword snatch and the takedown — with @sketch holding the scale in every frame.

**Assets:** @Nate\_arena, @giant, @arena, @sketch

![image-5.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/a569f5d6-2172-4572-b335-f049f59318d8.png)

![hf_20260805_164601_f8fed2d9-cdb9-4e1a-a95b-2711bae5c35d.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/f1163d60-26b1-406b-8e4b-0eace2deafe8.png)

![image-7.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/ef45e051-8913-4e06-96be-f87c11aebbf9.png)

![hf_20260805_220717_8784cc00-d63a-4cd5-8f40-6e6ce64a235f.png](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/05598df2-aa8f-475c-ba5a-3481033d2669.png)

TITLE — MASTER SEQUENCE · RIDE & THROW → THE SWORD → THE FIGHT: one combined prompt, thirteen shots in strict order, every shot written at the same level of detail. The boy rides the chain on the giant's back and is slung off (shots 1–5); he lands right beside his sword and takes it (shot 6); the fight, Fury-Road style, two calf cuts and the two-stage fall (shots 7–13). 30 seconds total, real time throughout.

— REFERENCE DEFINITIONS (shared by the whole sequence) —

@c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0 — the boy. Young Central Asian warrior, early 20s, ~175 cm / 70 kg, short dark side-parted hair soaked with sweat, clean-shaven face with dirt and bruise smudges, sculpted bronze muscled cuirass over a ragged dark-red short-sleeved tunic, wide leather belt with a round bronze sun-medallion buckle, bronze bracers, pleated dark-red skirt with a frayed hem, tall gladiator sandals laced to the knees. Lean, light, fast. Character appearance only. WEAPON STATE: BOTH HANDS EMPTY through shots 1–5 and the start of shot 6 — his gladius was knocked away earlier and lies in the sand; from the snatch inside SHOT 6 onward the short gladius-style sword is in his RIGHT hand for the rest of the sequence.

@8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6 — the giant. Colossal executioner gladiator, ~4.5 m tall, several tons, immensely strong and slow to turn. FRONT: head hidden under a crudely stitched dark-brown leather hood with a wide leather mantle over the shoulders, round stitched eye-holes and a sutured mouth seam ON THE FRONT of the hood only, small glinting eyes deep inside; bare hairy chest, heavy sagging belly, thick fat-padded arms, short thick neck. BACK: the blank leather crown and nape of the hood with its vertical stitched seam (no eye-holes on this side); the scalloped rear edge of the mantle; mottled sun-weathered skin, shoulder blades flanking the chain crossing, spine furrow, leather waistband and torn rear hem of the skirt. CHAIN: ONE massive rusted chain, and only one — crossing in a large X in the middle of the bare BACK below the mantle, its four runs travelling downward and outward around the sides of the torso and under the armpits; it lies flat against the skin, wrapped tight, never passes over the tops of the shoulders, has NO hanging ends, nothing dangles from it; this is the ONLY chain that exists anywhere in the sequence. Ragged dark-brown hide skirt, crude leather sandals. HUGE long-hafted bearded battle axe with a rusted iron head in one fist — in rear view the axe reads frame-right, fixing that hand as the axe hand and the other as the FREE hand. Character appearance only. NOT inherited from the reference: grey studio backdrop, flat light, static pose, framing.

@727b9bb5-0d68-4e27-b9f0-6858d96ccd42 — the arena. Geometry, materials and atmosphere only: sun-bleached stone arena at midday, open sand, dust haze in the backlight, red-and-white perimeter wall, colonnade, roaring crowd behind the wall.

@b1a51747-8cec-44e4-aabb-22079a9952f1 — scale lock only: the giant is roughly two and a half times the boy's height; the boy's head barely reaches the giant's hip. Hold in every frame.

— GLOBAL STYLE, OPTICS, AUDIO (all shots) —

8K IMAX-scale photorealism, live-action footage from a real physical camera. Extreme anamorphic look: horizontal squeeze and compression, oval elliptical bokeh with stretched highlights, curved barrel edge distortion, mild chromatic aberration toward the corners — all restrained. NO lens flares, NO streaks, NO floating bokeh circles. 2.39:1, 24fps, 180° shutter motion blur. Colour 60:30:10 — sand and pale stone / dark iron and leather / dark-red accent. Natural light only: harsh midday desert sun, contre-jour, camera on the shadow side; sky blown out white; dust haze with sun shafts; light direction identical across the whole sequence. Pore-level skin realism, sweat through dust, rust transferring onto palms. Hollywood acting texture: micro-pauses before reactions, precise eye-lines, heavy breathing. NON-IP. — PHYSICS —

The leap: a real ballistic arc off the crater rim, path fixed once airborne, already falling as the hands meet the iron. The catch: a snatch at the limit of reach — fingertips first, hard shock load, knuckles blanching, the chain rolling a couple of centimetres across sweat-slick skin before it bites. The giant: everything initiates from the ground and hips and cannot be recalled mid-motion; sandals sink and plow furrows; flesh lags a beat behind the skeleton; he is never destabilised by 70 kg. The rotation: angular momentum, one revolution, slow to build, heavy through the middle; the boy is thrown by the SUDDEN STOP, released along the tangent. The axe blows in the fight: full commitment, real craters, the boy thrown clear only by his own timed evasions. The falls: the landing tumble dumps momentum ugly and involuntary; the giant's fall is two-stage biomechanics — knee first, then the mass tipping belly-up. The sand craters and sprays ballistically; dust persists shot to shot; correct contact shadows always, including the flight shadow in shot 2 and the giant's long shadow carrying a small human shape in the wides.

— CONSTRAINTS —

Exactly two figures on the sand in all thirteen shots; the crowd stays behind the perimeter wall. Exactly ONE chain exists — crossed flat in an X on the giant's back, no loose ends, never duplicated, never a weapon. In shots 1–5 the boy is on the giant's BACK, never his front; the catch happens ON THE CUT into shot 3; the giant's hands never touch the boy anywhere in the sequence. The boy's hands are empty until the snatch in SHOT 6; from then on the gladius stays in his right hand. In the fight his strikes land ONLY on the BACKS of the giant's calves, from behind, exactly twice, each leaving a thin blood-seamed scar — dark clotted blood held inside the cut, beading and staining its edges, never flowing or dripping — each followed by a scream AFTER the visible contact. The giant's fall is knee-first, then belly-up, and he stays alive, breathing, fighting the pain. The giant keeps hood, mantle and axe-hand consistent across all shots. The boy travels left to right through the entire sequence. All shots handheld except SHOTS 3 and 6.

le+edge%2C+shoulder+blades+flanking+the+chain+crossing%2C+spine+furrow%2C+mottled+skin%2C+rear+waistband+and+rear+hem%2C+backs+of+the+arms+with+elbow+points+at+camera%29%3B+front+markers+%28chest%2C+belly%2C+groin%2C+eye-holes%2C+mouth+seam%2C+face+of+the+hood%29+do+NOT+appear+in+shots+1%E2%80%933.+SHOT+4+is+the+one+sanctioned+shot+showing+the+front+of+the+hood%2C+in+three-quarter%2C+with+the+boy+invisible+behind+the+bulk.+POSTURE%3A+the+giant+fully+upright%2C+vertical+spine%2C+never+bent+or+staggered+by+70+kg.+GRIP+POINT%3A+the+X-crossing+of+the+chain+between+the+SHOULDER+BLADES%2C+%7E2.8+m+up+%E2%80%94+fingers+jammed+under+the+crossed+links%3B+the+only+handhold.+THE+REACH+FAILS%3A+fat%2C+thick%2C+relatively+short+arms+and+a+short+thick+neck+%E2%80%94+he+physically+cannot+reach+the+middle+of+his+own+back%3B+fingers+close+on+air%2C+short+by+a+clear+margin%2C+every+time%3B+that+is+why+he+resorts+to+rotation.+SCREEN+DIRECTION%3A+the+boy+travels+LEFT+TO+RIGHT+through+the+entire+sequence.+FIGHT+RULES+%28shots+7%E2%80%9313%29%3A+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+fights+like+the+HULK+from+the+Avengers+%E2%80%94+a+wrathful+berserk+colossus+with+explosive+smash+energy%2C+yet+a+seasoned+champion+who+almost+NEVER+misses%3A+every+axe+blow+and+fist+strike+is+precise%2C+fully+committed+and+devastating%2C+cratering+the+sand+exactly+where+it+is+aimed%2C+so+the+boy+survives+by+centimeters.+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E+fights+like+JOHN+WICK+%E2%80%94+a+cold+surgical+professional%3A+deft%2C+attentive%2C+economical%2C+reading+each+attack+a+breath+early%2C+dodging+with+the+minimum+necessary+movement%3B+his+sword+answers+only+at+the+giant%27s+legs+%E2%80%94+exactly+TWO+cuts+across+the+BACKS+of+the+calves%2C+delivered+from+behind%2C+each+leaving+a+thin+dark+scar+line+SEAMED+WITH+BLOOD%3A+the+blood+sits+IN+the+cut%2C+dark+and+clotted%2C+beaded+along+the+line+and+staining+its+edges+%E2%80%94+it+stays+inside+the+wound%2C+never+running%2C+never+dripping%2C+never+spattering.+Each+cut+is+followed+by+the+giant%27s+scream+of+pain.+Strike+and+evasion+always+synchronized+as+one+cause-and-effect+pair%3A+the+giant+aims+at+the+exact+spot+where+the+boy+IS%2C+and+the+boy%27s+move+carries+him+away+from+THAT+precise+blow.+Medium+frames+on+the+giant%3B+only+the+boy%27s+face+may+go+closer%3B+the+skin+everywhere+else+stays+clean+%E2%80%94+the+only+blood+in+the+sequence+lives+inside+those+two+cuts.%0A%0A%E2%80%94+ACTION+TIMING+%C2%B7+13+SHOTS+%E2%80%94%0A%0ASHOT+1+%C2%B7+0.0%E2%80%931.0s+%C2%B7+THE+TARGET+%E2%80%94+handheld%2C+low+%28%7E0.9+m%29%2C+running+BEHIND+AND+JUST+LEFT+of+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E%27s+shoulder%2C+aimed+forward+at+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+across+the+%3C%3C%3C727b9bb5-0d68-4e27-b9f0-6858d96ccd42%3E%3E%3E+sand.+The+boy%27s+back+and+right+shoulder+fill+the+near+foreground-left%2C+both+hands+empty%2C+arms+driving%3B+ahead+across+the+churned+sand+the+giant+stands+upright%2C+squarely+FROM+BEHIND+as+in+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+%E2%80%94+blank+crown+of+the+hood%2C+scalloped+mantle%2C+the+chain%27s+X+catching+the+sun%2C+axe+hanging+in+his+fist+%E2%80%94+hood+turned+away%2C+scanning+the+far+side+of+the+arena.+A+SHORT+shot%3A+two-three+driving+strides%2C+the+camera+jolting+with+each%3B+the+boy+plants+a+sandal+on+the+raised+rim+of+an+old+axe+crater+and+leaves+the+ground.+Shot+ends+as+his+feet+come+off+the+rim.+His+eyes+are+locked+on+the+chain+crossing+from+the+first+stride.%0A%0ASHOT+2+%C2%B7+1.0%E2%80%932.5s+%C2%B7+THE+LEAP+IN+PROFILE+%E2%80%94+wide%2C+handheld%2C+camera+low+%28%7E1.0+m%29+to+the+SIDE+and+slightly+behind+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E%3A+the+giant+in+three-quarter+REAR+view+at+frame-right+per+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E%2C+full+4.5+m+in+frame%3B+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E%27s+flight+arc+crosses+the+frame+in+PROFILE+from+frame-left+%E2%80%94+quick+and+compact%2C+body+extended%2C+arms+reaching+for+the+crossed+links%2C+legs+trailing%2C+skirt+hem+snapping%2C+his+shadow+racing+across+the+sand+into+the+giant%27s%2C+the+flight+in+near-silhouette+against+the+backlight.+CUT+at+the+last+instant+BEFORE+contact+%E2%80%94+fingertips+a+hand%27s+width+from+the+links%3B+the+catch+is+not+in+this+shot.%0A%0ASHOT+3+%C2%B7+2.5%E2%80%934.5s+%C2%B7+THE+CATCH+AND+THE+FACE+%E2%80%94+WIDE+from+ABOVE+in+3%2F4+on+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E%2C+camera+high+over+the+giant%27s+shoulder+line+looking+down+past+the+blank+nape+and+mantle+onto+the+back%3A+the+hands+SLAM+onto+the+X-crossing+on+the+cut-in%2C+read+as+impact+%E2%80%94+body+swinging+through+and+slapping+flat+against+the+bare+back%2C+knees+sinking+into+the+fat%2C+dust+jumping+off+the+skin%2C+the+giant+jolting%2C+the+chain+grinding+link+on+link%3B+his+legs+swing+free+over+the+drop.+His+upturned+FACE+stays+readable+%E2%80%94+the+acting+beat+plays+through+his+eyes+in+this+order%3A+a+darting+glance+AROUND%2C+one+flick+each+way+across+the+arena+far+below%3B+then+the+gaze+CLIMBS+the+chain+link+by+link+along+the+taut+crossed+runs%2C+up+toward+the+mantle+and+the+blank+stitched+nape+%E2%80%94+and+arrives+at+what+the+chain+is+wrapped+AROUND%3A+the+giant+himself.+The+arrival+is+a+freeze+%E2%80%94+pupils+stop%2C+breath+catches%2C+BEWILDERMENT+flooding+the+upturned+face+between+his+straining+arms.+%28Safety%3A+gaze+always+engaged%2C+alive+and+focused%3B+natural+blink+cadence.%29%0A%0ASHOT+4+%C2%B7+4.5%E2%80%936.5s+%C2%B7+THREE-QUARTER+%E2%80%94+HE+TRIES+TO+SEE+IT+%E2%80%94+MEDIUM%2C+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+in+3%2F4+front+view%3A+the+stitched+eye-holes+and+sutured+mouth+seam+readable+at+the+angle%2C+the+heavy+chest%2C+the+axe+fist+hanging+still.+Both+arms+stay+DOWN+%E2%80%94+no+swinging%2C+no+reaching.+He+tries+to+SEE+what+has+climbed+onto+him%3A+the+hooded+head+cranks+over+one+shoulder+as+far+as+the+short+thick+neck+allows%2C+torso+twisting+a+little+after+it+%E2%80%94+ONCE+to+the+RIGHT+%E2%80%94+then+whips+back+and+cranks+over+the+other+shoulder+%E2%80%94+ONCE+to+the+LEFT+%E2%80%94+each+turn+falling+short%2C+the+eye-holes+never+getting+the+back+into+view%2C+the+mantle+creasing%2C+the+chain+shifting+on+his+skin.+The+boy+stays+INVISIBLE+behind+the+bulk+%E2%80%94+only+a+jolted+flash+of+an+elbow+or+sandal+past+the+silhouette%27s+edge.+The+muffled+bellow+builds%2C+more+baffled+than+enraged.%0A%0ASHOT+5+%C2%B7+6.5%E2%80%939.5s+%C2%B7+ONE+REVOLUTION+AND+THE+SNAP+%E2%80%94+SUPER-WIDE+from+GROUND+LEVEL%2C+camera+in+the+sand%3A+the+whole+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+against+the+blown-out+sky+and+colonnade.+ONE+full+heavy+revolution+around+his+own+axis+%E2%80%94+sandals+shuffling+and+stamping+a+tight+circle%2C+a+ring+of+dust+punching+outward%2C+belly+and+chest+fat+lagging+a+beat+behind+the+skeleton%2C+the+axe+swinging+wide+on+the+outside+of+the+turn%2C+the+boy+clamped+to+the+rotating+back%2C+hidden+and+revealed+as+the+bulk+comes+around+%E2%80%94+then+he+PLANTS+one+sandal+hard+and+STOPS+the+several-ton+torso+dead+against+it.+The+sudden+stop+does+everything%3A+the+boy%27s+inertia+rips+his+fingers+from+the+links+and+he+is+SLUNG+off+on+the+tangent+toward+frame-right%2C+a+streak+of+motion+blur%2C+and+exits+the+frame+right.+The+chain+snaps+flat+back+with+a+heavy+rattle%3B+the+giant+is+left+planted+and+heaving+in+his+own+dust+wall.+His+hands+never+touch+the+boy.%0A%0ASHOT+6+%C2%B7+9.5%E2%80%9313.5s+%C2%B7+THE+LANDING+AT+THE+SWORD+%E2%80%94+static+ground-level+camera%2C+35mm%2C+locked+off%2C+one+focus+rack%3B+the+landing+and+the+pickup+are+ONE+shot.+At+the+start+the+frame+holds+ONLY+sand+and+the+sword%3A+the+short+gladius+lies+completely+FLAT+on+its+side+in+the+foreground+third%2C+hilt+toward+camera%2C+every+centimetre+of+blade+and+hilt+resting+on+the+surface%2C+lightly+dusted%2C+nothing+pointing+at+the+sky.+FOCUS+sits+on+the+open+patch+of+sand+deeper+in+frame+%E2%80%94+the+exact+spot+where+he+is+about+to+land+%E2%80%94+the+sword+soft+in+the+foreground.+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E+is+SLUNG+IN+from+the+LEFT+edge+low+and+fast%2C+crashes+shoulder-first+into+that+focused+patch+and+the+momentum+carries+him+through+a+tumbling+GROUND+ROLL+%E2%80%94+shoulder%2C+hip%2C+shoulder+%E2%80%94+that+dumps+him+right+BESIDE+the+blade%2C+at+ground+level%2C+sand+blasting+up.+Dazed%2C+propped+on+his+hands%3A+LOST+and+FRIGHTENED%2C+breath+panicked%2C+quick+jumpy+glances+around%2C+understanding+nothing+%E2%80%94+until+his+eyes+CATCH+the+sword+a+hand+away.+The+FOCUS+RACKS+from+the+landing+spot+onto+the+BLADE+%E2%80%94+and+inside+that+sword-focused+frame+the+change+lands%3A+one+beat+of+stillness%2C+the%0A%0Ajaw+sets%2C+the+eyes+harden%2C+resolve+replacing+fear.+He+SNATCHES+the+sword+off+the+sand+in+one+sharp+move%2C+rises+and+sprints+OUT+of+frame+toward+frame-RIGHT+%E2%80%94+toward+the+unseen+giant.+The+giant+never+appears+in+this+shot%3B+the+camera+never+moves%2C+only+the+focus+shifts+once.%0A%0ASHOT+7+%C2%B7+13.5%E2%80%9315.5s+%C2%B7+THE+CHARGE+%E2%80%94+low+angle+from+the+sand%2C+directly+BEHIND+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E%2C+handheld+pursuit+energy%3A+the+boy+runs+away+from+camera+across+the+open+sand+toward+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E%2C+who+stands+mid-arena+against+the+backlit+colonnade+and+blown-out+sky%2C+dust+rolling+around+his+feet%2C+the+huge+axe+in+his+fist%2C+sword+now+in+the+boy%27s+right+hand%2C+sand+kicking+off+his+heels+%E2%80%94+his+small+dark+running+figure+center-framed+against+the+giant%27s+silhouette%2C+growing+with+every+stride.%0A%0ASHOT+8+%C2%B7+15.5%E2%80%9317.5s+%C2%B7+FIRST+BLOOD+OF+THE+SAND+%E2%80%94+the+first+exchange%2C+Fury-Road+cut%3A+the+giant+meets+the+charge+with+a+full+overhead+axe+blow+%E2%80%94+the+camera+CRASH-CAMS+into+the+impact+as+the+iron+head+buries+into+the+sand+and+detonates+a+crater+EXACTLY+where+the+boy+was+a+half-second+ago%2C+the+boy%27s+dodge-roll+carrying+him+around+the+giant%27s+flank+in+the+same+beat%2C+dead+CENTER-FRAMED+through+the+whole+move%2C+dust+wall+blasting+across+the+lens+and+grains+staying+smeared+on+the+glass.%0A%0ASHOT+9+%C2%B7+17.5%E2%80%9319.0s+%C2%B7+THE+FIRST+CUT+%E2%80%94+the+camera+FALLS+onto+the+ATTACK+LINE+behind+the+boy%27s+shoulder%2C+shooting+along+his+back+toward+the+giant%27s+calf%2C+the+depth+swallowing+the+distance%2C+his+body+closing+the+angle+%E2%80%94+and+we+clearly+SEE+the+swipe+LAND+across+the+BACK+of+the+calf+muscle%2C+the+moment+of+contact+readable+in+frame%3B+a+long+thin+SCAR+line+stays+across+the+calf%2C+seamed+with+dark+clotted+blood+%E2%80%94+beaded+along+the+cut%2C+staining+its+edges%2C+held+inside+the+wound+without+a+single+run+or+drip+%E2%80%94+and+the+instant+AFTER+the+visible+hit+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+SCREAMS+in+pain.+The+frame+CUTS+on+the+peak+of+the+swing.%0A%0ASHOT+10+%C2%B7+19.0%E2%80%9321.0s+%C2%B7+THE+PRICE+%E2%80%94+MEDIUM+on+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E%2C+center-framed%2C+the+frame+trembling+with+his+roar%3A+hooded+head%2C+shoulders+and+chest+together+in+the+shot+%E2%80%94+he+is+still+screaming%2C+the+small+eyes+inside+the+stitched+eye-holes+crushing+into+fury+%E2%80%94+and+inside+the+same+shot+he+wheels+his+whole+mass+around+after+the+boy%2C+hauling+the+axe+up+out+of+the+crater%2C+dust+avalanching+off+his+shoulders%2C+the+wounded+leg+already+betraying+a+hitch+in+his+stance.%0A%0ASHOT+11+%C2%B7+21.0%E2%80%9324.0s+%C2%B7+THE+BARRAGE+%E2%80%94+fast+center-framed+cutting%2C+a+new+shot+every+beat%2C+the+camera+rushing+with+the+boy%27s+rolls+like+a+pursuit+vehicle+and+whipping+around+the+giant+like+passing+wreckage%3A+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+unloads+a+SERIES+%E2%80%94+overhead+chops+and+wide+horizontal+sweeps%2C+each+aimed+at+the+exact+spot+where+the+boy+IS%2C+each+cratering+the+sand+and+detonating+walls+of+dust+%E2%80%94+and+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E+escapes+each+specific+blow+with+its+own+specific+evasion%2C+a+tumbling+roll+under+the+sweep%2C+a+last-instant+sidestep+off+the+chop%2C+always+half+a+beat+ahead%2C+sweat+flying%2C+breath+ragged%2C+micro+speed-ups+giving+the+giant%27s+blows+a+jolting+feral+energy%3B+ochre+sand+and+white+sky+burning+like+the+Fury+Road+desert.%0A%0ASHOT+12+%C2%B7+24.0%E2%80%9325.5s+%C2%B7+THE+SECOND+CUT+%E2%80%94+on+the+last+evasion+the+boy+rounds+the+giant%27s+back+once+more%3B+again+the+camera+lands+on+the+ATTACK+LINE+behind+his+shoulder%2C+depth+flattening+the+distance%2C+his+body+closing+the+angle+%E2%80%94+and+we+clearly+SEE+the+blade+LAND+across+the+BACK+of+the+OTHER+calf%2C+a+second+thin+scar+line+seamed+with+dark+clotted+blood+%E2%80%94+beaded+in+the+cut%2C+staining+its+edges%2C+staying+inside+the+wound+without+running%3B+%3C%3C%3C8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6%3E%3E%3E+SCREAMS+in+pain+the+instant+after+the+visible+contact.+The+frame+CUTS+at+the+strike%27s+peak....%0A%0ASHOT+13+%C2%B7+25.5%E2%80%9330.0s+%C2%B7+THE+TWO-STAGE+FALL+AND+THE+HOLD+%E2%80%94+wide%2C+center-framed%3A+the+freshly+cut+leg+BUCKLES+and+the+giant+drops+onto+THAT+SAME+KNEE%2C+screaming%2C+the+axe+falling+from+his+grip+into+the+sand+%E2%80%94+a+held+breath%2C+folded+by+the+pain+%E2%80%94+and+then+the+whole+body+topples+over+onto+his+BACK%2C+BELLY+UP%3A+a+several-ton%2C+frame-shaking+crash%2C+a+rolling+dust+wave+washing+toward+the+lens%2C+the+chain+rattling+flat+against+the+sand.+He+lies+breathing+hard%2C+his+huge+belly+and+ribs+rising+and+falling%2C+jaw+clenched+behind+the+leather+hood%2C+visibly+fighting+the+pain.+In+the+FAR+THIRD%0A%0Aof+the+composition+stands+exhausted+%3C%3C%3Cc6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0%3E%3E%3E+%E2%80%94+winded%2C+shoulders+slightly+hunched+with+fatigue%2C+chest+heaving%2C+sword+lowered%2C+watching+the+defeated+giant.+Dust+settles%3B+the+crowd%27s+roar+swells+to+the+end+of+the+clip.%0A%0A%E2%80%94+PHYSICS+%E2%80%94%0AThe+leap%3A+a+real+ballistic+arc+off+the+crater+rim%2C+path+fixed+once+airborne%2C+already+falling+as+the+hands+meet+the+iron.+The+catch%3A+a+snatch+at+the+limit+of+reach+%E2%80%94+fingertips+first%2C+hard+shock+load%2C+knuckles+blanching%2C+the+chain+rolling+a+couple+of+centimetres+across+sweat-slick+skin+before+it+bites.+The+giant%3A+everything+initiates+from+the+ground+and+hips+and+cannot+be+recalled+mid-motion%3B+sandals+sink+and+plow+furrows%3B+flesh+lags+a+beat+behind+the+skeleton%3B+he+is+never+destabilised+by+70+kg.+The+rotation%3A+angular+momentum%2C+one+revolution%2C+slow+to+build%2C+heavy+through+the+middle%3B+the+boy+is+thrown+by+the+SUDDEN+STOP%2C+released+along+the+tangent.+The+axe+blows+in+the+fight%3A+full+commitment%2C+real+craters%2C+the+boy+thrown+clear+only+by+his+own+timed+evasions.+The+falls%3A+the+landing+tumble+dumps+momentum+ugly+and+involuntary%3B+the+giant%27s+fall+is+two-stage+biomechanics+%E2%80%94+knee+first%2C+then+the+mass+tipping+belly-up.+The+sand+craters+and+sprays+ballistically%3B+dust+persists+shot+to+shot%3B+correct+contact+shadows+always%2C+including+the+flight+shadow+in+shot+2+and+the+giant%27s+long+shadow+carrying+a+small+human+shape+in+the+wides.%0A%0A%E2%80%94+CONSTRAINTS+%E2%80%94%0AExactly+two+figures+on+the+sand+in+all+thirteen+shots%3B+the+crowd+stays+behind+the+perimeter+wall.+Exactly+ONE+chain+exists+%E2%80%94+crossed+flat+in+an+X+on+the+giant%27s+back%2C+no+loose+ends%2C+never+duplicated%2C+never+a+weapon.+In+shots+1%E2%80%935+the+boy+is+on+the+giant%27s+BACK%2C+never+his+front%3B+the+catch+happens+ON+THE+CUT+into+shot+3%3B+the+giant%27s+hands+never+touch+the+boy+anywhere+in+the+sequence.+The+boy%27s+hands+are+empty+until+the+snatch+in+SHOT+6%3B+from+then+on+the+gladius+stays+in+his+right+hand.+In+the+fight+his+strikes+land+ONLY+on+the+BACKS+of+the+giant%27s+calves%2C+from+behind%2C+exactly+twice%2C+each+leaving+a+thin+blood-seamed+scar+%E2%80%94+dark+clotted+blood+held+inside+the+cut%2C+beading+and+staining+its+edges%2C+never+flowing+or+dripping+%E2%80%94+each+followed+by+a+scream+AFTER+the+visible+contact.+The+giant%27s+fall+is+knee-first%2C+then+belly-up%2C+and+he+stays+alive%2C+breathing%2C+fighting+the+pain.+The+giant+keeps+hood%2C+mantle+and+axe-hand+consistent+across+all+shots.+The+boy+travels+left+to+right+through+the+entire+sequence.+All+shots+handheld+except+SHOTS+3+and+6.)

* * *

### Scene 2 — The Queen's Box

**What it does:** Animates the acting half of the arena scene in 8 cuts: her hidden worry, the flirty exit, and the giant taking the ending back.

**Assets:** @Nate\_arena, @Aila\_arena, @giant, @arena, @throne, @sketch

![@Nate_arena](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/b8b4cf55-aee5-41bb-9a91-a462b8528a4a.png)

@Nate\_arena

![@Aila_arena](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/39ef2044-2001-4d74-891f-1c0b1b1f54c0.png)

@Aila\_arena

![@throne](https://d2ol7oe51mr4n9.cloudfront.net/user_3G85t64kByVQ9PWe1mWsRtmS5UX/c84f46d4-3493-473b-87e7-81a8d7533cca.png)

@throne

— REFERENCE DEFINITIONS —

@974124cf-65df-436c-8c38-4e4a9fdd6429 — the queen: long dark wavy hair, gold laurel wreath, white draped Grecian gown with a hammered gold corset-belt, gold cuff bracelets, delicate gold necklace, earrings. Face, hair and wardrobe 100% match. Regal but modest — wise, deep, interesting; no pomp.

@bfd35318-a2ba-4f1c-96ac-558f7bdab628 — the throne: a gilded carved throne with a tall arched back framing a round upholstered medallion, ornate gold scrollwork, boxy carved gilded armrests with velvet tops, heavy carved legs, deep wine-red velvet upholstery with an aged sheen. Match it 100%.

@c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0 — the boy, exactly his end-of-fight state: drenched in sweat, chest heaving with ragged breath, sand dust caked on wet skin, thin scrapes, short blade lowered in his right hand, sculpted bronze muscled cuirass over a ragged dark-red tunic, pleated dark-red skirt, bronze bracers, tall laced gladiator sandals. Exhausted, shoulders slightly hunched with fatigue.

@8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6 — colossal executioner gladiator, roughly two and a half times the boy's height (~4.5 m, several tons): head hidden under a crudely stitched dark-brown leather executioner's hood with round stitched eye-holes and a sutured mouth seam, small glinting eyes deep inside; bare hairy chest and belly, ONE massive rusted chain crossed flat on his back — the only chain in the scene; ragged dark-brown hide skirt, crude leather sandals; his HUGE bearded battle axe lies flat on the sand; two thin blood-seamed scar lines across the calves of both legs — dark clotted blood held inside the cuts, never running — the injury reading through the way the legs move.

@727b9bb5-0d68-4e27-b9f0-6858d96ccd42 — the arena: sun-bleached pale stone amphitheater under harsh desert sun, arched upper gallery, packed roaring tribunes, red-and-white plastered perimeter wall, dusty sand floor; the royal box is an elevated marble loggia with columns above the perimeter wall. Architecture, materials and light only; its own framing is not inherited.

@b1a51747-8cec-44e4-aabb-22079a9952f1 — scale lock only: the giant roughly two and a half times the boy's height. Hold in every frame where both appear.

THE ROYAL BOX, dressed and lived-in: the marble loggia with columns; a rich patterned CARPET across its floor; a low side TABLE beside the throne with dishes of fruit, a bowl of GRAPES and a jug and cup of WINE; sheer pale TULLE drapes between the columns, breathing in the hot wind. Royal GUARDS — dark armored silhouettes — behind her and at both sides, always in soft anamorphic-bokeh blur, never sharp, never acting. BOX GEOMETRY: the loggia sits BEHIND and ABOVE the ordinary spectator rows — the crowd is in FRONT of and BELOW her box; she watches over their heads, never among the crowd. The crowd is ALIVE and varied, never uniform — some leap up, some stay seated, some punch the air, some clutch their heads, waves of reaction rolling through the stands.

...— GLOBAL STYLE, OPTICS, AUDIO —

8K IMAX-scale photorealism, live-action footage from a real physical camera. Extreme anamorphic look — 2.39:1: horizontal squeeze and compression; oval / elliptical bokeh with stretched highlights; curved barrel edge distortion; chromatic aberration toward the edges — all subtle, gentle, moderate. NO lens flares, NO streaks, NO floating bokeh circles. Fine visible film grain, slightly muted sun-bleached tones, gently pulled-down exposure, soft highlight rolloff. Natural light only — HARSH high desert sun in every shot, contre-jour where noted, crisp hard-edged shadows, dust haze glowing in the beams. 24fps, 180° shutter motion blur. Colour 60:30:10 — sand and pale stone / dark iron, leather and shadow / dark-red and gold accents. Pore-level skin realism. Hollywood acting texture: micro-pauses before reactions, precise eye-lines, living eyes with catch-lights. Continuity: characters, props and environment identical across every cut; the throne identical in every appearance. Audio, environmental SFX only, pure diegetic: the living roar rising and falling, the collective shocked "OOOH", wind, the rustle of her gown, deep impacts, ragged breathing, drifting dust. No dialogue anywhere — the whole scene is

wordless.

SCENE EVENT — one event holds every character: THE UNCLAIMED VICTORY. She fears for him in secret; the crash she feared becomes his triumph; he tries to deliver the victory to her eyes; she withdraws them — with one private flirtatious smile — before it can land; the giant refuses the ending and takes it back with one punch. Nobody plays the event — everybody plays their own business inside it.

— ACTION TIMING · 8 CUTS —

CUT 1 · 0.0–3.5s · HER HIDDEN WORRY — eye-level CLOSE-UP 3/4 on @974124cf-65df-436c-8c38-4e4a9fdd6429, 75mm anamorphic, subtle slow push-in: behind her the roaring crowd dissolved into oval bokeh, tulle edges breathing at the frame sides, hard sun modeling her face. She watches the fight — eye-line forward and slightly down toward the center of the arena, below the frame line — calm, composed, quietly attentive; her stillness is warmth held in check, not marble. Then IT HAPPENS below: her breath catches — BARELY, hidden, almost imperceptible: no gasp, no jolt — just the smallest silent hold of breath, and her body eases a few centimeters FORWARD to see, a light quiet lean toward the arena, nothing in the face but a fraction more focus in the eyes; she is afraid for the one fighting down there and does NOT want it seen. Over this the crowd's roar SNAPS into a single shocked "OOOH" — the sound of something enormous landing.

ACTING TASK — @974124cf-65df-436c-8c38-4e4a9fdd6429: her face belongs to the crowd; her heart is a state secret. GOAL: guard him with her eyes alone while giving the stands nothing. The fear escapes into the body — the hidden breath-hold, the slight forward lean — while the face stays even; the work happens in her eyes. (Natural blink cadence, no frozen stare.)

CUT 2 · 3.5–6.0s · THE HAND ON THE ARMREST — tight SIDE-ON insert close-up along the throne, 75mm anamorphic, STATIC: her hand resting on the gilded scrolled ARMREST of @bfd35318-a2ba-4f1c-96ac-558f7bdab628, the velvet top and ornate gold carving sharp, the white gown and gold cuff bracelet at the edge, the crowd a far wall of bokeh beyond, hard sun glinting on the gold. Continuing the same moment: as the unseen exchange lands below, her hand does NOT move and does NOT close — it stays lying exactly where it is on the armrest, in the same open position — and simply TENSES: a light, quiet tightening passing through the hand, the tendons rising faintly under the skin, the fingertips pressing a touch deeper into the carved gold, nothing curling into a fist, knuckles never whitening — and then the tension holds. The entire worry of the scene lives in that one slight tensing of a hand that never changes position. Faces are not shown. The "OOOH" from the previous cut carries through. Hold on the tensed hand.

CUT 3 · 6.0–10.0s · THE OFFER — medium shot FROM BEHIND @c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0, 75mm anamorphic, slow push-in, sunbeams raking through the dust (the longest of his cuts): the shot OPENS on dust still RISING from under the fallen @8b44d49b-2e3a-41ef-a460-69f5fae03df6 — the giant has only just crashed down (the very fall the "OOOH" answered), the cloud of his impact still billowing around his huge body in the foreground. Over him, back to camera, stands @c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0: legs planted wide, torso LEANED slightly FORWARD over the fallen giant — the tilted, spent stance of a fighter one breath after the finish — back and shoulders heaving. Behind him the packed TRIBUNES: rows of spectators rising, applauding, the roar swelling; the royal box sits behind the camera. Slowly his gaze leaves the fallen opponent — his head comes around over his shoulder toward the camera into three-quarter, his eye-line settling just past the lens and slightly ABOVE the frame line — the queen sits on the tribunes right behind the camera. Scale per @b1a51747-8cec-44e4-aabb-22079a9952f1.

ACTING TASK — @c6381b92-738f-4532-ab37-c26c12055be0 (fully invested; the work happens in his eyes): MOTIVE: the whole fight was a message addressed to one pair of eyes; until she looks, the victory does not exist. GOAL: deliver the victory — find her eyes up there and collect the answer. OBSTACLE: his own body — the breath he cannot catch, the legs barely holding. TACTIC: his eyes climb straight to the one spot above the lens where she must

be and OFFER the win — here, I did it, look at me — then hold the offer up and wait, checking that exact spot for movement, for the white of her gown, for any answer at all. The exhaustion is never played — it leaks through the heaving shoulders and heavy lids while the eyes stay busy asking. (Safety: gaze always engaged; natural blink cadence.)

CUT 4 · 10.0–12.5s · SHE RISES — MEDIUM on the box in THREE-QUARTER view, STATIC, 50mm anamorphic: the loggia seen in 3/4 — the throne, the table with grapes and wine, the tulle, the blurred guards, hard sun cutting between the columns; the queen herself reads in 3/4, never frontal, never profile.
