2C+glances+down+the+next+gap+between+shoulders%2C+turns+her+head+to+take+the+row+on+her+other+side%2C+lifts+her+chin+to+see+past+the+heads.+Her+eyes+are+ALIVE%2C+BLINKING%2C+BRIGHT+AND+FULL+OF+HOPE+%E2%80%94+quick+and+expectant%2C+lifting+at+every+dark+coat+before+she+sees+it+is+a+stranger%2C+then+moving+on.+She+badly+wants+to+find+him.%0ANOT+ONE+spectator+notices+her%3A+every+body+she+passes+stays+turned+toward+the+show%2C+absorbed%2C+cheering+toward+the+centre+of+the+square%3B+nobody+glances+at+her%2C+nobody+turns+a+head%2C+nobody+makes+room.+The+HERO+is+NOWHERE+in+this+shot+%E2%80%94+not+in+frame%2C+not+in+the+blur%2C+nowhere.+Murmur+of+the+standing+crowd%2C+applause+rolling+in+from+the+centre%2C+distant+drums+%E2%80%94+no+music.%0ASHOT+2+%E2%80%94+ONE+CONTINUOUS+ORBIT+WITH+PUSH-IN%3A+medium+profile+%E2%86%92+super+close-up+frontal%3B+the+STOP%2C+the+ORBIT%27S+END+and+the+SILHOUETTE+land+in+ONE+MOMENT%2C+47%C2%B0+FOV+%284.5%E2%80%9311s%29%3A%0ACut+to+a+wider+scale+than+the+previous+shot%3A+it+opens+MEDIUM%2C+in+PROFILE%2C+her+whole+head+and+shoulders+and+the+gown%27s+bodice+in+frame%2C+the+standing+crowd%27s+backs+around+her+now+clearly+readable+rather+than+blurred+wipes+%E2%80%94+the+GIRL+%28match+%3C%3C%3C8d7e5287-98b7-444b-919f-ab85c7340877%3E%3E%3E%29+walking+through+the+dense+standing+mass+IN+A+SLIGHT+HURRY%2C+light+quick+steps+past+the+motionless+backs%2C+she+alone+in+motion%2C+slipping+through+the+gaps+without+jostling+and+without+touching+anyone.%0ASHE+IS+STILL+SEARCHING+as+she+walks%3A+she+keeps+turning+her+head+to+check+the+faces+she+passes%2C+glances+between+the+standing+bodies%2C+looks+along+the+rows+to+her+left+and+to+her+right%2C+still+hunting+him+among+the+masks.+But+the+search+is+RUNNING+OUT+%E2%80%94+her+looks+come+slower+and+hold+shorter%2C+each+one+answered+by+another+stranger%2C+and+under+them+a+light+upset+and+a+slight+sadness+surface+look+by+look%3A+the+hope+is+going+out+of+her+eyes+as+the+shot+progresses.+Her+eyes+stay+BLINKING+and+ALIVE+the+whole+time.%0AThe+camera+ORBITS+her+as+she+walks+AND+PUSHES+IN+at+the+same+time+%E2%80%94+gliding+around+from+her+profile+toward+her+front+while+CLOSING+from+medium+tighter+and+tighter%2C+the+crowd%27s+backs+sliding+past+the+frame+edges.+And+then+THREE+THINGS+HAPPEN+IN+THE+SAME+INSTANT%3A+the+orbit+SETTLES+FRONTAL+in+a+SUPER+CLOSE-UP%2C+her+face+filling+the+frame%2C+composed+on+the+golden+ratio+%E2%80%94+her+EYES+riding+the+UPPER+THIRD+LINE%2C+headroom+tight+and+deliberate+%E2%80%94+she+STOPS%2C+a+clean+full+stop+against+her+hurried+pace%2C+her+searching+cutting+off+mid-look+%E2%80%94+and+DEEP+BEHIND+HER%2C+in+the+soft+blur%2C+a+SILHOUETTE+separates+from+the+standing+crowd%3A+an+out-of-focus+dark+shape+of+a+man+in+a+long+coat+%28the+HERO%2C+match+%3C%3C%3C932441d0-4ea7-4982-a982-05486edcaa6a%3E%3E%3E%29%2C+unreadable%2C+moving+quietly+toward+her+back.+In+her+stop%2C+she+FEELS+him+near+%E2%80%94+and+HER+EYES+FILL+WITH+HOPE%3A+a+short+intake+of+breath%2C+her+chin+lifting+a+touch+%E2%80%94+she+does+not+turn%2C+she+stands+still+among+the+motionless+backs%2C+listening+with+her+whole+body.+She+does+not+see+him.+The+audience+does.+Around+her%2C+still+not+one+spectator+has+noticed+either+of+them.+Murmur+of+the+crowd%2C+far+cheers+and+drums%2C+her+breath+arriving+close+as+the+frame+tightens+%E2%80%94+no+music.%0ASHOT+3+%E2%80%94+GENTLE+SLOW+MOTION%2C+DETAIL+at+HIP+LEVEL%3A+her+BARE+right+hand+resting+by+her+hip+on+the+right+%E2%80%94+his+BARE+left+hand+reaches+DOWN+from+the+UPPER+LEFT%2C+INDEX+FINGER+leading%2C+and+its+fingertip+grazes+her+fingers%3B+the+film+ends+on+the+touch%2C+47%C2%B0+FOV+%2811%E2%80%9315s%29%3A%0AGENTLE+SLOW+MOTION+%E2%80%94+their+first+touch+deserves+it.+The+camera+sits+LOW+at+HIP+HEIGHT%2C+a+tight+DETAIL+frame+composed+on+the+thirds%3A+the+GIRL+on+the+RIGHT+of+the+frame%2C+her+body+and+burgundy+gown+set+on+the+RIGHT+THIRD+VERTICAL%2C+standing+still+%E2%80%94+her+BARE+RIGHT+hand+hanging+DOWN+ALONG+HER+BODY+beside+the+gown%27s+folds%2C+resting+naturally+against+her+hip%2C+the+way+a+hand+hangs+in+ordinary+life%3A+relaxed%2C+loose%2C+fingers+softly+curled%2C+arm+close+to+her+body+%E2%80%94+NOT+extended%2C+NOT+offered%2C+NOT+hovering+%E2%80%94+simply+a+hand+at+rest.+The+standing+crowd+reads+soft+in+the+blurred+background%2C+all+of+them+still+facing+away+toward+the+show.+From+the+UPPER+LEFT+of+the+frame+the+HERO%27s+BARE+LEFT+hand+ENTERS+%E2%80%94+reaching+in+a+slow+DIAGONAL+downward+toward+her+resting+hand%2C+his+INDEX+FINGER+LEADING%2C+extended+ahead+of+the+other+fingers%2C+the+others+relaxed+and+softly+curled+%E2%80%94+the+ONLY+moving+hand+in+the+frame%2C+unhurried%2C+certain+%E2%80%94+and+the+PAD+OF+HIS+INDEX+FINGER+GENTLY+GRAZES+her+fingers%3A+soft%2C+warm%2C+skin+to+skin+%E2%80%94+a+fingertip+touch%2C+not+a+grab%2C+the+first+contact+of+their+whole+story%2C+entirely+HIS+move%2C+the+point+of+contact+landing+on+a+GOLDEN-SECTION+INTERSECTION+of+the+frame.+A+slow+beat.+Only+now+her+fingers+ANSWER%2C+curling+softly+toward+his%2C+the+two+bare+hands+meeting+beside+the+burgundy+silk.+The+camera+HOLDS+on+the+meeting+hands+and+the+frame+stills.+Over+this+final+frame+the+carnival%27s+roar+THINS+AWAY+into+a+low+mechanical+hum+%E2%80%94+capped+by+a+single+bright+elevator+%22DING%22.+Silk%2C+breath%2C+distant+drums+fading+%E2%80%94+no+music.)

* * *

### Scene 6 — The Elevator

**What it does:** Animates the modern-day finale, with the hummed melody riding an attached voice-memo audio reference.

**Assets:** @Nate\_elevator, @Aila\_elavator, @elavator, @headphones1, @Audio

@Audio

SCENE CONTEXT

A 26-second, 3-shot quiet romantic elevator scene: SHOT 1 — the insert of the touch; SHOT 2 — the full first conversation up to the ding of her floor; SHOT 3 — outside: her smiling exit, his slow-draining face, the amazed line, the two-finger door save and the chase. Understated, alive, natural acting — no comedy mugging, no drama; HIS manner is calm, mature and slightly constrained — a quietly shy grown man, never a giddy teenager; the PAUSES are as important as the lines. All dialogue in ENGLISH, spoken QUIETLY — every line fully VOICED, never whispered; plus his single WORDLESS hummed line on @Audio 1's melody. All three shots STATIC, locked-off. 16:9. 26.0 s total.

ACTIVE REFERENCES

@f3a7f5f0-3dad-4421-a3bb-2653b2a9797c — elevator cab interior, reference shot from the door side: matte GREY stone-textured wall panels divided by thin steel seam strips; ONE slim steel handrail running in a single line across the back wall; a dark marble-speckled floor; a sectioned ceiling of warm glowing light panels in a dark grid frame, washing the cab in soft warm light; at the reference's left edge the dark door-side column with round backlit buttons. Controls materials, panels, handrail, floor, ceiling and light only — the reference's own WIDE framing is NOT inherited: shots 1-2 are framed much CLOSER, with no doorway and no button panel visible; shot 3 builds its own exterior framing (brushed-steel doors, plain matte light-beige wall, floor indicator).

@261e83df-223d-44be-80d4-e5c278fbf1b5 — NATE: young man, short dark hair, dark-grey linen shirt open with sleeves rolled over a light-grey t-shirt, blue jeans, sneakers, watch LEFT wrist, dark backpack RIGHT shoulder. Face, hair and wardrobe 100% match; ignore studio background. A calm, quietly reserved presence — simple and unpolished, never model-neat; a grown man, not a boy. HE IS TALL — long-limbed and lean, a small head on a tall frame; his height must read in every shot he appears in.

@5ce41c6b-9718-4a76-ab3f-f56dd681e418 — AYLA: young woman, long dark wavy hair loose, beige fine-knit cardigan open over a white top, thin belt, grey pleated short skirt, white socks and sneakers, small black bag on a thin strap LEFT shoulder, thin necklace. Face, hair and wardrobe 100% match; ignore studio background. ADD: the @b687a34f-7bac-4323-8b09-d2d6b36521b4 earbuds in her ears at the start, the thin white cord running down from both ears below the frame line.

@b687a34f-7bac-4323-8b09-d2d6b36521b4 — HER EARBUDS, 100% match: classic open-fit WHITE WIRED earbuds — small glossy white teardrop housings with an oval mesh grille on a thin round white cord, the two leads running a long way down to a small white Y-splitter with a slim white inline remote high on one lead. Never wireless, never in-ear silicone tips, never over-ear, never black, no logos or text. The cord hangs from her ears down her front and out of frame; no plug or cable end is ever visible.

@Audio 1 — the reference for the HUMMED LINE only, nothing else in the scene comes from it. Take from it ONLY the melody: the exact notes, pitches and intervals, the tempo, the phrasing and the rhythmic pause — note for note, beat for beat, no variation, nothing improvised. The line is sung WORDLESS, as "tu-tu" syllables landing exactly on those notes, and his lip-sync follows the melody's timing. Do NOT copy or imitate the voice, timbre or vocal identity heard in the recording — NATE hums in HIS OWN natural speaking voice, the same voice he speaks his lines with. All the spoken dialogue is performed as scripted below, not taken from any audio.

GEOGRAPHY AND GLOBAL LOCKS

Shots 1-2 INSIDE the moving cab: camera at the DOOR side at chest height, aimed frontally at the BACK wall — the grey stone-textured panels, steel seams and handrail line fill the background; the doors are BEHIND the camera and never visible, the button panel beside it, never visible. Shot 3 OUTSIDE: plain matte light-BEIGE wall around the brushed-steel doors, a small lit floor indicator above, empty hallway.

ABSOLUTE BODY LOCK (shots 1-2, every frame): both bodies FRONTAL — chests and shoulders SQUARE to camera, parallel to the back wall, feet planted; neither ever rotates torso, hips or shoulders toward the other — not for the wave, the handshake or any line; only heads quarter-turn, only eyes and hands carry the contact. NATE frame-LEFT, AYLA frame-RIGHT, a polite half-meter apart, never changing. His only extra motions: the few-centimeter forward lean of the opening, and the barely-there rhythm of the hummed line.

HEIGHT LOCK, all shots: NATE 200 cm, AYLA 170 cm — a genuinely TALL man, the top of her head only at his SHOULDER level, his eye line far above hers; to speak to her he tilts his head DOWN and she looks UP at him. In shot 2 his head rides high in the frame and hers noticeably lower; in shot 3 he reads as a visibly tall figure filling the cab doorway. Never flips, never equalizes, never shrinks between shots.

In shot 2 the frame line sits just below the chest — the handshake happens BELOW frame; only the wave is seen. Wardrobe locks: backpack right shoulder, watch left wrist, her bag left shoulder; both WIRED earbuds in at the start; she removes ONE with her LEFT hand, holds it on its cord, slips it back in during shot 3. ORDER OF THE OPENING: touch (shot 1) FIRST; the wave opens shot 2 as its direct continuation — the touch never repeats; earbud out NEXT; only THEN he speaks. Dialogue exactly as scripted, English, lip-synced. Static camera everywhere.

FORMAT

Three hard-cut shots, 26.0 s total, real time, each shot exactly its stated length. 16:9.

GLOBAL OPTICS AND STYLE

Shot on 35mm film, Kodak 500T tungsten stock: warm interior cast, fine visible grain, slightly muted tones, gently pulled-down exposure, soft highlight rolloff; no lens flares. Clean rectilinear framing.

SHOT 1 — 0.0-1.0s — INSERT CLOSE-UP: THE TOUCH, 47°, STATIC

First frame: static close-up in the warm-lit cab — her relaxed EMPTY hand at her side against the pleated skirt, the knit cuff of her cardigan above the wrist; NO earbud cord visible in this shot; his hand entering frame-LEFT, index finger extended toward the back of her hand.

Action: one quick honest beat — his fingertip gives the lightest, briefest tap to the BACK of her hand and pulls back a little too fast, self-conscious. Faces are not shown. Hard cut on the withdrawal.

SHOT 2 — 1.0-15.0s — MEDIUM CLOSE FRONTAL TWO-SHOT: THE CONVERSATION, 63°, STATIC

First frame: static MEDIUM CLOSE two-shot — both framed from just below the chest up, faces large; NATE frame-left, AYLA frame-right, bodies square to camera, his head clearly higher in frame than hers; behind them the grey stone-textured panels of @f3a7f5f0-3dad-4421-a3bb-2653b2a9797c, steel seams and handrail line; she stands carefree, both @b687a34f-7bac-4323-8b09-d2d6b36521b4 earbuds in, arms relaxed, gaze slightly UP at the indicator over the unseen doors; he beside her, still and composed, a quiet held-in presence, his near shoulder just settling back from the touch of shot 1 below frame.

Action — continuous, the pauses played fully as beats of their own; both bodies square to camera throughout:

— The shot opens as the DIRECT CONTINUATION of shot 1's touch — the same single touch, NEVER repeated; it reads here only through the small dip of his near shoulder below the frame and the tiny stir of her posture.

— THE WAVE (1.0-2.2s), immediately: body frontal, he raises his near hand in a small, low, RESTRAINED wave AIMED SIDEWAYS AT HER — a quiet minimal motion, palm toward her face, at the edge of her vision; a few-centimeter lean toward the doors allowed to slip it into her eyeline; a faint self-conscious half-smile with it, nothing more. HIS EYES ARE ON HER — his head makes a small quarter-turn and his gaze goes DOWN onto her face before the hand even rises, and STAYS there through the wave; never at his own hand, never forward at the camera.

— She notices, blinks out of the music, pulls out ONE earbud with her LEFT hand — it stays in her fingers on its white cord — and turns her head UP to him (head only), politely curious.

— Only now he speaks, LOOKING STRAIGHT AT HER, head tilted down toward her, calm but a little constrained, quiet and fully VOICED: "Hi." — simple, even, the voice a touch smaller than he meant. A tiny beat.

— Right after, he simply ASKS — hands down, no gesture: "What are you listening to?" — at HALF-VOICE, fully voiced, NOT a whisper; calm and simple, a beat slower than normal speech, only a faint warmth of flirt underneath — restrained, adult, a small quiet smile, his shy edge showing as evenness rather than play.

— She, a touch shy, half-smiling, matching his quiet: "Ah… it's Orynkhan." — pronounced "O-ryn-KHAN", crisp and natural.

— He, lightly surprised, his smile widening: "Oh, I love Orynkhan."

— She, amused, half-testing, half-teasing, with a smile: "Oh, really?"

— He gives no spoken reply — the song IS the answer. Recognizing the tune, something quietly settles in him: a soft small smile, and he SOFTLY HUMS ALONG WITH THE TUNE — calm, almost half-voice, even and UNHURRIED, in WORDLESS syllables: "Tu tutu tu tu tu tutu tu." — a small RHYTHMIC PAUSE, one beat held in the air — then "Tu tutu tu tu tututu tu tu." — and he HITS THE NOTES: ON-PITCH on @Audio 1's exact melody — same pitches, intervals, rhythm and pause, instantly recognizable, sung along as if the song plays in his head — but the VOICE never from the recording: his OWN natural voice, the same one he speaks with, never imitating its timbre, never rushed; no lyrics, only tu-tu syllables riding the tune. The very first note carries a breath of shyness — a touch soft, almost checking himself — steadying by the second phrase, still quiet, now sure of the notes.

THE PERFORMANCE, almost none: he stands QUIET and still through the hum — at most the smallest nod in time and fingertips resting on the backpack strap; no head-bob show, no shoulder pops, no play-acting; the stillness itself reads as his shyness.

THE GAZE: on the opening phrase he glances aside, as if hearing the song in his head; on the KEY second phrase his eyes come down and settle calmly INTO HERS — warm, steady, quietly brave for a constrained man — HELD to the end of the line. The hum trails off into a faint self-conscious smile and one almost-silent exhale of a laugh, eyes still on her.

— She LAUGHS — a bright, genuine, delighted little laugh, warmer than anything before it; the loudest moment of this quiet scene, still sized for a small room — and it doesn't end alone: they SHARE a light FLIRTY laughter, a couple of soft laughs traded back and forth, eyes finding each other inside it — playful, charmed, never loud — his breathy laugh under and after hers.

— THE HELD LOOK (one-to-two full seconds): the laugh settles into an awkward pause — his eyes, on her since the key phrase, STAY, and she looks back up at him: both hold it openly; his nervous smile settles into a real one, hers stays warm; neither breaks early; the cab hum fills the silence.

— Out of that look, quieter, simpler: "I'm Nate." — his RIGHT hand offered below the frame line (his near hand, the one closest to her — NEVER the left), read from the shoulders, torso never turning.

— She takes it lightly with her RIGHT hand (the earbud staying in her LEFT fingers): "Ayla. Nice to meet you." — pronounced "EYE-lah", never "Ay-EE-la" — an awkward smiling little nod; the brief RIGHT-handed handshake below frame, both bodies frontal.

— One more small shared beat — and the elevator BELL RINGS: a soft, clearly audible DING; she looks UP-AND-FORWARD, straight ahead and just above the lens — at the floor indicator OVER THE DOORS — a purposeful glance at a real thing, NOT a vague ceiling look: "Oh, that's my floor." — the LAST line of the conversation, finished COMPLETELY inside this shot, the doors still closed, nobody moving to exit.

Hold half a second on the two of them frontal, the sentence landing on him. Hard cut. THE DIALOGUE IS OVER BEFORE THE CUT — nothing of it carries into shot 3.

SHOT 3 — 15.0-26.0s — MEDIUM-WIDE FROM OUTSIDE: HER EXIT, HIS LINE, THE TWO FINGERS, 63°, STATIC

This shot begins ONLY AFTER the conversation has fully ended: the DING has rung and her "Oh, that's my floor." has been spoken back in shot 2 — the doors open for the FIRST time here, never earlier.

Action, unhurried, the pauses given full room:

\- NATE stays in the open cab, smile still on — then THE SLOW CHANGE: as she disappears it drains by degrees into a light puzzlement — a barely-there knit of the brow — and settles into a BLANK, emotionless stun: the face quiet and still, eyes fixed where she went, nothing performed, a switched-off stillness. Doors stay open on him. A real PAUSE on this empty face.

\- Then, to himself, syllable by syllable, in a NORMAL SPEAKING VOICE — plainly voiced, amazed, conversational volume, NOT a whisper: "Ho-ly shit…" — with CLEAR, PRONOUNCED mouth articulation, more readable than normal speech: the lips visibly shape every syllable — the rounded "Ho", the parted "ly", the teeth-and-lip "shit" — deliberate, slightly over-enunciated, unmistakable on camera, never a frozen mouth.

Cut on his first quick steps.

ACTING

Scene event (unspoken): the small brave act of starting — a first conversation neither will admit they wanted; "romance" is never performed, it lives in the pauses and the eyes.

NATE — a GROWN MAN, calm and composed on the surface, shy underneath — not a teenager, nothing boyish, nothing comedic. His acting is SIMPLE and QUIET: he keeps himself small and still, spends words carefully, and the shyness reads as RESTRAINT — held-in, a little constrained: a beat of hesitation before each line, lines landing quieter than planned, one micro glance-away, a soft swallow before the question — and then stillness again; SUBTLE, never overplayed, never fidgety. Motive: he has ridden ten floors rehearsing one word. Goal: a name exchange before her floor. His body refuses the easy move — he cannot turn toward her, so everything travels through the lean, the wave, the eyes. Tactic — courage spent in small coins, quietly; the hummed line is the one big coin, and even that he spends calmly, not showily. THE BREAK: her laugh at his hummed tune — inside the song the tightness eases a degree, the melody lends a quiet steadiness rather than swagger; her laugh lands while his eyes are already on her, and for the first time they STAY; out of the held look comes the simplest line he owns: "I'm Nate." In shot 3 the arc lives on his face alone: the smile slowly DRAINS — light puzzlement, then a blank emotionless stun, the face switching off; "Ho-ly shit…" is not comedy — a private exhale escaping the blankness; the two-finger save is instinct outrunning shyness; the half-shout cracks slightly, more hope than volume.

AYLA — motive: the ride was hers — her music, her morning; but a stranger who dared to speak is a rare event, and she's honest enough to be curious. Goal: find out, giving nothing away, whether he's worth the second earbud. Tactic — measured hospitality: one earbud for the wave, short answers, "Oh, really?" as a light dare; his bashfulness amuses her. THE BREAK: the timid one suddenly humming to her — the laugh escapes before she approves it, and she meets the held look instead of deflecting. In shot 3 she exits SMILING — the warm private smile carried out of frame, the earbud going back in, the day resuming, pleasantly; she never looks back.

3A+touch+%28shot+1%29+FIRST%3B+the+wave+opens+shot+2+as+its+direct+continuation+%E2%80%94+the+touch+never+repeats%3B+earbud+out+NEXT%3B+only+THEN+he+speaks.+Dialogue+exactly+as+scripted%2C+English%2C+lip-synced.+Static+camera+everywhere.%0AFORMAT%0AThree+hard-cut+shots%2C+26.0+s+total%2C+real+time%2C+each+shot+exactly+its+stated+length.+16%3A9.%0AGLOBAL+OPTICS+AND+STYLE%0AShot+on+35mm+film%2C+Kodak+500T+tungsten+stock%3A+warm+interior+cast%2C+fine+visible+grain%2C+slightly+muted+tones%2C+gently+pulled-down+exposure%2C+soft+highlight+rolloff%3B+no+lens+flares.+Clean+rectilinear+framing.%0ASHOT+1+%E2%80%94+0.0-1.0s+%E2%80%94+INSERT+CLOSE-UP%3A+THE+TOUCH%2C+47%C2%B0%2C+STATIC%0AFirst+frame%3A+static+close-up+in+the+warm-lit+cab+%E2%80%94+her+relaxed+EMPTY+hand+at+her+side+against+the+pleated+skirt%2C+the+knit+cuff+of+her+cardigan+above+the+wrist%3B+NO+earbud+cord+visible+in+this+shot%3B+his+hand+entering+frame-LEFT%2C+index+finger+extended+toward+the+back+of+her+hand.%0AAction%3A+one+quick+honest+beat+%E2%80%94+his+fingertip+gives+the+lightest%2C+briefest+tap+to+the+BACK+of+her+hand+and+pulls+back+a+little+too+fast%2C+self-conscious.+Faces+are+not+shown.+Hard+cut+on+the+withdrawal.%0ASHOT+2+%E2%80%94+1.0-15.0s+%E2%80%94+MEDIUM+CLOSE+FRONTAL+TWO-SHOT%3A+THE+CONVERSATION%2C+63%C2%B0%2C+STATIC%0AFirst+frame%3A+static+MEDIUM+CLOSE+two-shot+%E2%80%94+both+framed+from+just+below+the+chest+up%2C+faces+large%3B+NATE+frame-left%2C+AYLA+frame-right%2C+bodies+square+to+camera%2C+his+head+clearly+higher+in+frame+than+hers%3B+behind+them+the+grey+stone-textured+panels+of+%3C%3C%3Cf3a7f5f0-3dad-4421-a3bb-2653b2a9797c%3E%3E%3E%2C+steel+seams+and+handrail+line%3B+she+stands+carefree%2C+both+%3C%3C%3Cb687a34f-7bac-4323-8b09-d2d6b36521b4%3E%3E%3E+earbuds+in%2C+arms+relaxed%2C+gaze+slightly+UP+at+the+indicator+over+the+unseen+doors%3B+he+beside+her%2C+still+and+composed%2C+a+quiet+held-in+presence%2C+his+near+shoulder+just+settling+back+from+the+touch+of+shot+1+below+frame.%0AAction+%E2% +his+smile+widening%3A+%22Oh%2C+I+love+Orynkhan.%22%0A%E2%80%94+She%2C+amused%2C+half-testing%2C+half-teasing%2C+with+a+smile%3A+%22Oh%2C+really%3F%22%0A%E2%80%94+He+gives+no+spoken+reply+%E2%80%94+the+song+IS+the+answer.+Recognizing+the+tune%2C+something+quietly+settles+in+him%3A+a+soft+small+smile%2C+and+he+SOFTLY+HUMS+ALONG+WITH+THE+TUNE+%E2%80%94+calm%2C+almost+half-voice%2C+even+and+UNHURRIED%2C+in+WORDLESS+syllables%3A+%22Tu+tutu+tu+tu+tu+tutu+tu.%22+%E2%80%94+a+small+RHYTHMIC+PAUSE%2C+one+beat+held+in+the+air+%E2%80%94+then+%22Tu+tutu+tu+tu+tututu+tu+tu.%22+%E2%80%94+and+he+HITS+THE+NOTES%3A+ON-PITCH+on+%40Audio+1%27s+exact+melody+%E2%80%94+same+pitches%2C+intervals%2C+rhythm+and+pause%2C+instantly+recognizable%2C+sung+along+as+if+the+song+plays+in+his+head+%E2%80%94+but+the+VOICE+never+from+the+recording%3A+his+OWN+natural+voice%2C+the+same+one+he+speaks+with%2C+never+imitating+its+timbre%2C+never+rushed%3B+no+lyrics%2C+only+tu-tu+syllables+riding+the+tune.+The+very+first+note+carries+a+breath+of+shyness+%E2%80%94+a+touch+soft%2C+almost+checking+himself+%E2%80%94+steadying+by+the+second+phrase%2C+still+quiet%2C+now+sure+of+the+notes.%0ATHE+PERFORMANCE%2C+almost+none%3A+he+stands+QUIET+and+still+through+the+hum+%E2%80%94+at+most+the+smallest+nod+in+time+and+fingertips+resting+on+the+backpack+strap%3B+no+head-bob+show%2C+no+shoulder+pops%2C+no+play-acting%3B+the+stillness+itself+reads+as+his+shyness.%0ATHE+GAZE%3A+on+the+opening+phrase+he+glances+aside%2C+as+if+hearing+the+song+in+his+head%3B+on+the+KEY+second+phrase+his+eyes+come+down+and+settle+calmly+INTO+HERS+%E2%80%94+warm%2C+steady%2C+quietly+brave+for+a+constrained+man+%E2%80%94+HELD+to+the+end+of+the+line.+The+hum+trails+off+into+a+faint+self-conscious+smile+and+one+almost-silent+exhale+of+a+laugh%2C+eyes+still+on+her.%0A%E2%80%94+She+LAUGHS+%E2%80%94+a+bright%2C+genuine%2C+delighted+little+laugh%2C+warmer+than+anything+before+it%3B+the+loudest+moment+of+this+quiet+scene%2C+still+sized+for+a+small+room+%E2%80%94+and+it+doesn%27t+end+alone%3A+they+SHARE+a+light+FLIRTY+laughter%2C+a+couple+of+soft+laughs+traded+back+and+forth%2C+eyes+finding+each+other+inside+it+%E2%80%94+playful%2C+charmed%2C+never+loud+%E2%80%94+his+breathy+laugh+under+and+after+hers.%0A%E2%80%94+THE+HELD+LOOK+%28one-to-two+full+seconds%29%3A+the+laugh+settles+into+an+awkward+pause+%E2%80%94+his+eyes%2C+on+her+since+the+key+phrase%2C+STAY%2C+and+she+looks+back+up+at+him%3A+both+hold+it+openly%3B+his+nervous+smile+settles+into+a+real+one%2C+hers+stays+warm%3B+neither+breaks+early%3B+the+cab+hum+fills+the+silence.%0A%E2%80%94+Out+of+that+look%2C+quieter%2C+simpler%3A+%22I%27m+Nate.%22+%E2%80%94+his+RIGHT+hand+offered+below+the+frame+line+%28his+near+hand%2C+the+one+closest+to+her+%E2%80%94+NEVER+the+left%29%2C+read+from+the+shoulders%2C+torso+never+turning.%0A%E2%80%94+She+takes+it+lightly+with+her+RIGHT+hand+%28the+earbud+staying+in+her+LEFT+fingers%29%3A+%22Ayla.+Nice+to+meet+you.%22+%E2%80%94+pronounced+%22EYE-lah%22%2C+never+%22Ay-EE-la%22+%E2%80%94+an+awkward+smiling+little+nod%3B+the+brief+RIGHT-handed+handshake+below+frame%2C+both+bodies+frontal.%0A%E2%80%94+One+more+small+shared+beat+%E2%80%94+and+the+elevator+BELL+RINGS%3A+a+soft%2C+clearly+audible+DING%3B+she+looks+UP-AND-FORWARD%2C+straight+ahead+and+just+above+the+lens+%E2%80%94+at+the+floor+indicator+OVER+THE+DOORS+%E2%80%94+a+purposeful+glance+at+a+real+thing%2C+NOT+a+vague+ceiling+look%3A+%22Oh%2C+that%27s+my+floor.%22+%E2%80%94+the+LAST+line+of+the+conversation%2C+finished+COMPLETELY+inside+this+shot%2C+the+doors+still+closed%2C+nobody+moving+to+exit.%0AHold+half+a+second+on+the+two+of+them+frontal%2C+the+sentence+landing+on+him.+Hard+cut.+THE+DIALOGUE+IS+OVER+BEFORE+THE+CUT+%E2%80%94+nothing+of+it+carries+into+shot+3.%0ASHOT+3+%E2%80%94+15.0-26.0s+%E2%80%94+MEDIUM-WIDE+FROM+OUTSIDE%3A+HER+EXIT%2C+HIS+LINE%2C+THE+TWO+FINGERS%2C+63%C2%B0%2C+STATIC%0AThis+shot+begins+ONLY+AFTER+the+conversation+has+fully+ended%3A+the+DING+has+rung+and+her+%22Oh%2C+that%27s+my+floor.%22+has+been+spoken+back+in+shot+2+%E2%80%94+the+doors+open+for+the+FIRST+time+here%2C+never+earlier.%0AFirst+frame%3A+static+MEDIUM-WIDE+general+view+from+OUTSIDE+the+elevator%2C+camera+at+chest+height%2C+square+and+frontal+on+the+still-CLOSED+brushed-steel+doors%2C+which+sit+centred+and+take+up+roughly+the+central+third+of+the+frame+width%3B+plain+matte+light-BEIGE+walls+to+either+side+running+out+to+the+frame+edges+%E2%80%94+smooth+evenly+painted+beige+plaster%2C+flat+and+unremarkable%2C+NO+tiles%2C+no+stone+cladding%2C+no+marble%2C+no+glossy+surface%2C+no+pattern%3B+a+small+lit+floor+indicator+above+the+doors%3B+the+hallway+floor+plain+and+matte%2C+in+frame%3B+empty+hallway.+A+beat+on+the+closed+doors+%E2%80%94+then+they+slide+open.%0AAction%2C+unhurried%2C+the+pauses+given+full+room%3A%0A-+The+doors+slide+open+%E2%80%94+both+inside%2C+frontal%2C+still+smiling%3B+NATE+reads+FULL-LENGTH+in+the+doorway%2C+head+to+sneakers%2C+a+visibly+TALL+figure+with+clear+space+above+his+head%3B+AYLA+steps+out+SMILING+%E2%80%94+a+warm+private+smile+carried+with+her+%E2%80%94+and+walks+off+frame-RIGHT%2C+unhurried%2C+slipping+the+wired+earbud+back+in+as+she+goes%3B+she+does+NOT+look+back.%0A-+NATE+stays+in+the+open+cab%2C+smile+still+on+%E2%80%94+then+THE+SLOW+CHANGE%3A+as+she+disappears+it+drains+by+degrees+into+a+light+puzzlement+%E2%80%94+a+barely-there+knit+of+the+brow+%E2%80%94+and+settles+into+a+BLANK%2C+emotionless+stun%3A+the+face+quiet+and+still%2C+eyes+fixed+where+she+went%2C+nothing+performed%2C+a+switched-off+stillness.+Doors+stay+open+on+him.+A+real+PAUSE+on+this+empty+face.%0A-+Then%2C+to+himself%2C+syllable+by+syllable%2C+in+a+NORMAL+SPEAKING+VOICE+%E2%80%94+plainly+voiced%2C+amazed%2C+conversational+volume%2C+NOT+a+whisper%3A+%22Ho-ly+shit%E2%80%A6%22+%E2%80%94+with+CLEAR%2C+PRONOUNCED+mouth+articulation%2C+more+readable+than+normal+speech%3A+the+lips+visibly+shape+every+syllable+%E2%80%94+the+rounded+%22Ho%22%2C+the+parted+%22ly%22%2C+the+teeth-and-lip+%22shit%22+%E2%80%94+deliberate%2C+slightly+over-enunciated%2C+unmistakable+on+camera%2C+never+a+frozen+mouth.%0A-+The+doors+slide+slowly+closed+%E2%80%94+and+get+almost+ALL+THE+WAY%3A+the+gap+narrows+to+a+few+centimeters%2C+a+breath+from+sealing+%E2%80%94+only+THEN+his+TWO+fingers+shoot+into+the+sliver+from+inside%3B+the+doors+catch+and+obediently+reopen%2C+and+he+hurries+out+after+her+frame-right%2C+half-shouting%3A+%22Ayla%2C+wait%21%22+Cut+on+his+first+quick+steps.%0AACTING%0AScene+event+%28unspoken%29%3A+the+small+brave+act+of+starting+%E2%80%94+a+first+conversation+neither+will+admit+they+wanted%3B+%22romance%22+is+never+performed%2C+it+lives+in+the+pauses+and+the+eyes.%0ANATE+%E2%80%94+a+GROWN+MAN%2C+calm+and+composed+on+the+surface%2C+shy+underneath+%E2%80%94+not+a+teenager%2C+nothing+boyish%2C+nothing+comedic.+His+acting+is+SIMPLE+and+QUIET%3A+he+keeps+himself+small+and+still%2C+spends+words+carefully%2C+and+the+shyness+reads+as+RESTRAINT+%E2%80%94+held-in%2C+a+little+constrained%3A+a+beat+of+hesitation+before+each+line%2C+lines+landing+quieter+than+planned%2C+one+micro+glance-away%2C+a+soft+swallow+before+the+question+%E2%80%94+and+then+stillness+again%3B+SUBTLE%2C+never+overplayed%2C+never+fidgety.+Motive%3A+he+has+ridden+ten+floors+rehearsing+one+word.+Goal%3A+a+name+exchange+before+her+floor.+His+body+refuses+the+easy+move+%E2%80%94+he+cannot+turn+toward+her%2C+so+everything+travels+through+the+lean%2C+the+wave%2C+the+eyes.+Tactic+%E2%80%94+courage+spent+in+small+coins%2C+quietly%3B+the+hummed+line+is+the+one+big+coin%2C+and+even+that+he+spends+calmly%2C+not+showily.+THE+BREAK%3A+her+laugh+at+his+hummed+tune+%E2%80%94+inside+the+song+the+tightness+eases+a+degree%2C+the+melody+lends+a+quiet+steadiness+rather+than+swagger%3B+her+laugh+lands+while+his+eyes+are+already+on+her%2C+and+for+the+first+time+they+STAY%3B+out+of+the+held+look+comes+the+simplest+line+he+owns%3A+%22I%27m+Nate.%22+In+shot+3+the+arc+lives+on+his+face+alone%3A+the+smile+slowly+DRAINS+%E2%80%94+light+puzzlement%2C+then+a+blank+emotionless+stun%2C+the+face+switching+off%3B+%22Ho-ly+shit%E2%80%A6%22+is+not+comedy+%E2%80%94+a+private+exhale+escaping+the+blankness%3B+the+two-finger+save+is+instinct+outrunning+shyness%3B+the+half-shout+cracks+slightly%2C+more+hope+than+volume.%0AAYLA+%E2%80%94+motive%3A+the+ride+was+hers+%E2%80%94+her+music%2C+her+morning%3B+but+a+stranger+who+dared+to+speak+is+a+rare+event%2C+and+she%27s+honest+enough+to+be+curious.+Goal%3A+find+out%2C+giving+nothing+away%2C+whether+he%27s+worth+the+second+earbud.+Tactic+%E2%80%94+measured+hospitality%3A+one+earbud+for+the+wave%2C+short+answers%2C+%22Oh%2C+really%3F%22+as+a+light+dare%3B+his+bashfulness+amuses+her.+THE+BREAK%3A+the+timid+one+suddenly+humming+to+her+%E2%80%94+the+laugh+escapes+before+she+approves+it%2C+and+she+meets+the+held+look+instead+of+deflecting.+In+shot+3+she+exits+SMILING+%E2%80%94+the+warm+private+smile+carried+out+of+frame%2C+the+earbud+going+back+in%2C+the+day+resuming%2C+pleasantly%3B+she+never+looks+back.%0A%28Gazes+task-engaged%2C+natural+blink+cadence%3B+the+held+look+warm%2C+never+a+frozen+stare.%29%0APHYSICS+%2F+AUDIO%0AReal+elevator+life%3A+faint+hum+and+sway%3B+the+earbud+removal+one+clean+motion%2C+the+cord+swinging%3B+the+handshake+brief+below+frame+%E2%80%94+his+RIGHT+hand+to+her+RIGHT+hand%3B+the+elevator+BELL+%E2%80%94+a+soft%2C+clearly+audible+DING+%E2%80%94+rings+near+the+end+of+shot+2%2C+BEFORE+her+last+line+and+long+before+the+doors+open%3B+in+shot+3+the+doors+slide+with+motor+whirr+and+rubber-seal+sound%2C+his+two+fingers+physically+stop+the+door+edge+in+the+last+centimeters%2C+the+safety+reopen+kicks+in%3B+her+steps+soft+in+the+hallway.+Audio%3A+quiet+cab+tone+under+the+scene%3B+faint+tinny+music+from+her+earbuds+until+one+is+out%3B+all+spoken+lines+exactly+as+scripted%2C+QUIET%2C+fully+VOICED%2C+never+whispered%3B+the+hummed+line+ON-PITCH+on+%40Audio+1%27s+exact+notes%2C+intervals%2C+tempo+and+pause+%E2%80%94+wordless+%22tu-tu%22+syllables+in+HIS+OWN+speaking+voice%2C+soft%2C+true+to+the+tune%2C+never+an+imitation+of+the+recording%27s+voice%3B+her+laugh+bright+and+short%2C+then+a+brief+exchange+of+light+flirty+laughs%2C+never+hysterical%3B+%22Ho-ly+shit%E2%80%A6%22+at+NORMAL+conversational+volume%2C+amazed%2C+never+hushed%3B+the+half-shout+%22Ayla%2C+wait%21%22+with+light+hallway+echo%3B+pauses+near-silent%3B+no+score%2C+narration+or+subtitles.%0AQUALITY%0ASharp+readable+faces%2C+natural+micro+motion+inside+the+static+frames%2C+accurate+English+lip-sync+%E2%80%94+with+clearly+visible+articulation+on+%22Ho-ly+shit%E2%80%A6%22+%E2%80%94+no+ghosting%2C+duplicated+limbs%2C+flicker+or+camera+drift.+SKIN%2C+both%3A+living+TEXTURED+natural+skin+%E2%80%94+visible+pores%2C+micro-imperfections%2C+subtle+tonal+variation%3B+no+smoothing%2C+beauty-retouch+or+plastic+CGI%3B+a+natural+healthy+sheen+under+the+warm+light%2C+never+oily%2C+never+flat+matte.%0ACONSTRAINTS%0ATwo+people%3B+empty+hallway.+HEIGHT%3A+he+200+cm%2C+she+170+%E2%80%94+a+striking+30+cm+difference%2C+her+head+at+his+shoulder%2C+and+he+reads+as+a+visibly+TALL+man+in+every+shot%3B+never+equalized+or+flipped.+All+shots+static+locked-off.+Shots+1-2%3A+no+doors%2C+doorway+or+button+panel+visible%3B+the+reference%27s+wide+angle+never+reproduced%3B+ABSOLUTE+BODY+LOCK+%E2%80%94+both+bodies+square+to+camera+every+frame%2C+only+heads%2C+eyes+and+hands+move%3B+he+frame-left%2C+she+frame-right%2C+half+a+meter+apart%3B+the+wave+aims+sideways+at+her%2C+never+at+the+camera%2C+and+his+eyes+are+on+her+from+the+wave+onward.+The+touch+happens+ONCE+%28shot+1%29%3B+shot+2+opens+as+its+continuation+and+never+repeats+it.+She+removes+ONE+wired+earbud+with+her+LEFT+hand+and+keeps+it+on+its+cord+until+shot+3%3B+the+earbuds+never+become+wireless.+Sustained+eye+contact+only+AFTER+the+hummed+line+and+the+shared+laughter.+THE+HUM+IS+ON-PITCH%3A+notes+match+%40Audio+1%27s+melody+EXACTLY+%E2%80%94+same+pitches%2C+intervals%2C+rhythm+and+pause%2C+instantly+recognizable+%E2%80%94+the+voice+HIS+OWN%2C+never+the+recording%27s.+His+manner+is+that+of+a+MATURE+man+%E2%80%94+calm%2C+simple%2C+a+little+constrained%3B+the+shyness+stays+LIGHT+and+quiet%2C+never+overplayed%2C+never+boyish%2C+never+comedic.+The+dialogue+is+exactly+the+scripted+English+lines+plus+the+WORDLESS+hummed+line%2C+nothing+added%3B+%22Ayla%22+is+pronounced+%22EYE-lah%22.+HARD+ORDER%3A+shot+1+%28the+touch%29+%E2%86%92+shot+2+%28the+ENTIRE+dialogue%2C+ending+with+the+DING+and+her+last+line%29+%E2%86%92+only+THEN+shot+3+outside%2C+where+the+doors+open+for+the+first+time%3B+no+line+of+the+conversation+is+spoken+in+shot+3%2C+and+the+doors+never+open+during+shot+2.+In+shot+3+the+doors+stay+OPEN+through+his+pause+and+line%2C+then+close+almost+COMPLETELY+%E2%80%94+a+few-centimeter+gap+%E2%80%94+before+his+TWO+fingers+dart+in+at+the+last+instant%2C+never+early%3B+she+exits+frame-right+smiling%2C+never+looks+back%3B+the+only+shot-3+lines+are+%22Ho-ly+shit%E2%80%A6%22+and+%22Ayla%2C+wait%21%22.+The+piece+ends+on+his+first+quick+steps+out.)

* * *

That's the whole pipeline: lock the assets, let the skill write the prompts, generate in batches and stitch the best takes in the edit. Swap in your own photos and make one for your friends' wedding — or turn it into your first paid gig.
